+import { BadRequestException, ForbiddenException, Injectable, NotFoundException, OnModuleInit } from '@nestjs/common';
+import { InjectRepository } from '@nestjs/typeorm';
+import { DataSource, IsNull, Repository } from 'typeorm';
+import { OrganiserReputationEntity } from '../../entities/organiser-reputation.entity';
+import { OrganiserReputationHistoryEntity } from '../../entities/organiser-reputation-history.entity';
+import { OrganiserSanctionEntity } from '../../entities/organiser-sanction.entity';
+import { PrizePoolExceptionEntity } from '../../entities/prize-pool-exception.entity';
+import { PlatformSettingEntity } from '../../entities/platform-setting.entity';
+import { NotificationsService } from '../notifications/notifications.service';
+import { AuditLogService } from '../../common/audit/audit.service';
+import {
+  DEFAULT_GLOBAL_MAX_PRIZE_POOL,
+  QUALIFYING_THRESHOLDS,
+  RECOVERY_COMPETITIONS_REQUIRED,
+  RED_BAN_DAYS,
+  REPUTATION_LEVEL_LIMITS,
+  REPUTATION_LEVELS,
+  ReputationLevel,
+  SanctionType,
+  isReputationLevel,
+} from '../../common/reporting';
+
+export interface ReputationFacts {
+  qualifyingAmount?: number;
+  paidOnTime?: boolean;
+  seriousMisconduct?: boolean;
+  mediumViolations?: number;
+  overduePayments?: number;
+  outstandingObligations?: number;
+  failedPaymentDuringRecovery?: boolean;
+  involvedInValidDispute?: boolean;
+  reason?: string;
+  reportId?: string;
+}
+
+const levelRank = (level: ReputationLevel) => REPUTATION_LEVELS.indexOf(level);
+
+@Injectable()
+export class ReputationService implements OnModuleInit {
+  constructor(
+    @InjectRepository(OrganiserReputationEntity)
+    private readonly reputation: Repository<OrganiserReputationEntity>,
+    @InjectRepository(OrganiserReputationHistoryEntity)
+    private readonly history: Repository<OrganiserReputationHistoryEntity>,
+    @InjectRepository(OrganiserSanctionEntity)
+    private readonly sanctions: Repository<OrganiserSanctionEntity>,
+    @InjectRepository(PrizePoolExceptionEntity)
+    private readonly exceptions: Repository<PrizePoolExceptionEntity>,
+    @InjectRepository(PlatformSettingEntity)
+    private readonly settings: Repository<PlatformSettingEntity>,
+    private readonly dataSource: DataSource,
+    private readonly notifications: NotificationsService,
+    private readonly auditLog: AuditLogService,
+  ) {}
+
+  async onModuleInit(): Promise<void> {
+    try {
+      await this.ensureDefaults();
+    } catch (err) {
+      // The database may not be reachable during boot; migrations seed the same row.
+      console.warn('Reputation defaults deferred:', (err as Error).message);
+    }
+  }
+
+  private async ensureDefaults(): Promise<void> {
+    const existing = await this.settings.findOne({ where: { key: 'max_prize_pool' } });
+    if (!existing) {
+      await this.settings.save(this.settings.create({ key: 'max_prize_pool', value: String(DEFAULT_GLOBAL_MAX_PRIZE_POOL), updatedBy: 'system' }));
+    }
+  }
+
+  /** Platform-wide ceiling (never bypassable, not even by an exception). */
+  async getGlobalMax(): Promise<number> {
+    const row = await this.settings.findOne({ where: { key: 'max_prize_pool' } });
+    const parsed = row ? Number(row.value) : NaN;
+    return Number.isFinite(parsed) && parsed > 0 ? parsed : DEFAULT_GLOBAL_MAX_PRIZE_POOL;
+  }
+
+  async setGlobalMax(value: number, updatedBy: string): Promise<number> {
+    if (!Number.isFinite(value) || value <= 0) {
+      throw new BadRequestException('The platform maximum prize pool must be a positive amount.');
+    }
+    if (value > 10_000_000) {
+      throw new BadRequestException('The platform maximum prize pool cannot exceed Γé╣1,00,00,000.');
+    }
+    await this.settings.upsert({ key: 'max_prize_pool', value: String(Math.round(value)), updatedBy }, ['key']);
+    await this.auditLog.record('PLATFORM_PRIZE_CEILING_UPDATED', {
+      actor: updatedBy,
+      entityType: 'platform_setting',
+      entityId: 'max_prize_pool',
+      details: `Platform prize-pool ceiling set to Γé╣${Math.round(value).toLocaleString('en-IN')}.`,
+      metadata: { value: Math.round(value) },
+    });
+    return Math.round(value);
+  }
+
+  /** Normalises a row that may predate the counter defaults (or was inserted directly). */
+  private normalize(profile: OrganiserReputationEntity): OrganiserReputationEntity {
+    (profile as any).qualifyingAbove50k = Number((profile as any).qualifyingAbove50k || 0);
+    (profile as any).qualifyingAbove7k = Number((profile as any).qualifyingAbove7k || 0);
+    (profile as any).qualifyingAbove5k = Number((profile as any).qualifyingAbove5k || 0);
+    (profile as any).orangeRecoveryCount = Number((profile as any).orangeRecoveryCount || 0);
+    (profile as any).paidOnTimeCount = Number((profile as any).paidOnTimeCount || 0);
+    (profile as any).overduePaymentCount = Number((profile as any).overduePaymentCount || 0);
+    (profile as any).mediumViolationCount = Number((profile as any).mediumViolationCount || 0);
+    (profile as any).outstandingObligations = Number((profile as any).outstandingObligations || 0);
+    (profile as any).banCount = Number((profile as any).banCount || 0);
+    (profile as any).lifetimeBan = !!(profile as any).lifetimeBan;
+    return profile;
+  }
+
+  async getOrCreate(username: string): Promise<OrganiserReputationEntity> {
+    const existing = await this.reputation.findOne({ where: { username } });
+    if (existing) return this.normalize(existing);
+    const created = this.reputation.create({ username, level: 'blue' });
+    try {
+      return this.normalize(await this.reputation.save(created));
+    } catch (err) {
+      const raced = await this.reputation.findOne({ where: { username } });
+      if (raced) return this.normalize(raced);
+      throw err;
+    }
+  }
+
+  private effectiveLimit(level: ReputationLevel, globalMax: number): number {
+    return Math.min(REPUTATION_LEVEL_LIMITS[level], globalMax);
+  }
+
+  /** Full organiser reputation profile, including history and sanctions. */
+  async getProfile(username: string) {
+    const profile = await this.getOrCreate(username);
+    const globalMax = await this.getGlobalMax();
+    const [levelHistory, sanctionRows, pendingExceptions] = await Promise.all([
+      this.history.find({ where: { username }, order: { createdAt: 'DESC' }, take: 50 }),
+      this.sanctions.find({ where: { username }, order: { createdAt: 'DESC' }, take: 50 }),
+      this.exceptions.find({ where: { organiserUsername: username }, order: { createdAt: 'DESC' }, take: 20 }),
+    ]);
+    const now = Date.now();
+    const activeSanctions = sanctionRows.filter(
+      (s) => s.active && (!s.endsAt || new Date(s.endsAt).getTime() > now) && !['formal_warning', 'final_warning'].includes(s.type),
+    );
+    return {
+      username,
+      level: profile.level,
+      levelLabel: profile.level,
+      limit: this.effectiveLimit(profile.level, globalMax),
+      levelLimit: REPUTATION_LEVEL_LIMITS[profile.level],
+      globalMax,
+      counters: {
+        qualifyingAbove50k: profile.qualifyingAbove50k,
+        qualifyingAbove7k: profile.qualifyingAbove7k,
+        qualifyingAbove5k: profile.qualifyingAbove5k,
+        paidOnTimeCount: profile.paidOnTimeCount,
+        overduePaymentCount: profile.overduePaymentCount,
+        mediumViolationCount: profile.mediumViolationCount,
+        outstandingObligations: profile.outstandingObligations,
+        orangeRecoveryCount: profile.orangeRecoveryCount,
+      },
+      bannedUntil: profile.bannedUntil,
+      banCount: profile.banCount,
+      lifetimeBan: profile.lifetimeBan,
+      activeSanctions,
+      history: levelHistory,
+      sanctions: sanctionRows,
+      exceptions: pendingExceptions,
+      updatedAt: profile.updatedAt,
+    };
+  }
+
+  /** Throws when the organiser may not create/manage competitions right now. */
+  async assertCanOrganise(username: string): Promise<OrganiserReputationEntity> {
+    const profile = await this.getOrCreate(username);
+    if (profile.lifetimeBan) {
+      throw new ForbiddenException('This organiser is permanently banned from organising competitions.');
+    }
+    if (profile.bannedUntil && new Date(profile.bannedUntil).getTime() > Date.now()) {
+      throw new ForbiddenException(
+        `This organiser is serving a competition-creation ban until ${new Date(profile.bannedUntil).toISOString()}.`,
+      );
+    }
+    const now = Date.now();
+    const blocking = await this.sanctions.find({ where: { username, active: true } });
+    const current = blocking.find(
+      (s) =>
+        ['creation_restriction', 'one_month_ban', 'lifetime_ban', 'competition_suspension'].includes(s.type) &&
+        (!s.endsAt || new Date(s.endsAt).getTime() > now),
+    );
+    if (current) {
+      throw new ForbiddenException(
+        `A platform restriction is active on this account (${current.type.replace(/_/g, ' ')}): ${current.reason}`,
+      );
+    }
+    return profile;
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * Central prize-pool gate. Applies the reputation limit, the configurable
+   * platform ceiling and (when needed) a single-use approved exception.
+   */
+  async authorizePrizePool(amount: number, username: string) {
+    const profile = await this.getOrCreate(username);
+    const globalMax = await this.getGlobalMax();
+    const levelLimit = REPUTATION_LEVEL_LIMITS[profile.level];
+    const limit = this.effectiveLimit(profile.level, globalMax);
+
+    if (!Number.isFinite(amount) || amount < 0) {
+      throw new BadRequestException('Prize pool must be a valid, non-negative amount.');
+    }
+    if (amount > globalMax) {
+      throw new BadRequestException(
+        `The platform-wide maximum prize pool is Γé╣${globalMax.toLocaleString('en-IN')}. A competition cannot exceed this ceiling, even with an exception.`,
+      );
+    }
+    if (amount <= limit) {
+      return { level: profile.level, levelLimit, globalMax, limit, exceptionId: null as string | null };
+    }
+
+    const exception = await this.exceptions.findOne({
+      where: { organiserUsername: username, status: 'approved', competitionId: IsNull() },
+      order: { createdAt: 'ASC' },
+    });
+    if (exception && Number(exception.requestedAmount) >= amount) {
+      return { level: profile.level, levelLimit, globalMax, limit, exceptionId: exception.id };
+    }
+
+    throw new BadRequestException(
+      `Your current reputation level (${profile.level}) permits a maximum prize pool of Γé╣${limit.toLocaleString('en-IN')}. ` +
+        `The platform maximum is Γé╣${globalMax.toLocaleString('en-IN')}. Request a prize-pool exception from an administrator to run a competition above your limit.`,
+    );
+  }
+
+  /** Binds an approved single-use exception to the competition that used it. */
+  async consumeException(exceptionId: string, competitionId: string, amount: number): Promise<void> {
+    const result = await this.exceptions
+      .createQueryBuilder()
+      .update(PrizePoolExceptionEntity)
+      .set({ competitionId })
+      .where('id = :id AND status = :status AND "competitionId" IS NULL AND "requestedAmount" >= :amount', {
+        id: exceptionId,
+        status: 'approved',
+        amount,
+      })
+      .execute();
+    if (!result.affected) {
+      throw new BadRequestException('The approved prize-pool exception is no longer available.');
+    }
+  }
+
+  async requestException(username: string, requestedAmount: number, reason: string) {
+    const globalMax = await this.getGlobalMax();
+    if (!Number.isFinite(requestedAmount) || requestedAmount <= 0) {
+      throw new BadRequestException('Requested prize-pool amount must be a positive number.');
+    }
+    if (requestedAmount > globalMax) {
+      throw new BadRequestException(`An exception can never exceed the platform maximum of Γé╣${globalMax.toLocaleString('en-IN')}.`);
+    }
+    if (!reason || reason.trim().length < 10) {
+      throw new BadRequestException('Explain why the exception is needed (at least 10 characters).');
+    }
+    const record = await this.exceptions.save(
+      this.exceptions.create({ organiserUsername: username, requestedAmount: Math.round(requestedAmount), reason: reason.trim(), status: 'pending' }),
+    );
+    await this.auditLog.record('PRIZE_EXCEPTION_REQUESTED', {
+      actor: username,
+      entityType: 'prize_pool_exception',
+      entityId: record.id,
+      details: `Requested a prize-pool exception for Γé╣${Math.round(requestedAmount).toLocaleString('en-IN')}.`,
+      metadata: { requestedAmount },
+    });
+    return record;
+  }
+
+  async listExceptions(filter: { organiserUsername?: string; status?: string }) {
+    const where: Record<string, any> = {};
+    if (filter.organiserUsername) where.organiserUsername = filter.organiserUsername;
+    if (filter.status) where.status = filter.status;
+    return this.exceptions.find({ where, order: { createdAt: 'DESC' }, take: 200 });
+  }
+
+  async reviewException(id: string, status: 'approved' | 'rejected', note: string, reviewer: string) {
+    const record = await this.exceptions.findOne({ where: { id } });
+    if (!record) throw new NotFoundException('Prize-pool exception not found');
+    if (record.status !== 'pending') throw new BadRequestException('This exception has already been reviewed.');
+    const globalMax = await this.getGlobalMax();
+    if (status === 'approved' && Number(record.requestedAmount) > globalMax) {
+      throw new BadRequestException(`Cannot approve an amount above the platform maximum of Γé╣${globalMax.toLocaleString('en-IN')}.`);
+    }
+    record.status = status;
+    record.reviewer = reviewer;
+    record.reviewNote = note || null;
+    const saved = await this.exceptions.save(record);
+    await this.auditLog.record(status === 'approved' ? 'PRIZE_EXCEPTION_APPROVED' : 'PRIZE_EXCEPTION_REJECTED', {
+      actor: reviewer,
+      entityType: 'prize_pool_exception',
+      entityId: id,
+      details: `${status} Γé╣${Number(record.requestedAmount).toLocaleString('en-IN')} for @${record.organiserUsername}.`,
+      metadata: { note: note || null },
+    });
+    await this.notifications
+      .create({
+        toUsername: record.organiserUsername,
+        type: 'prize-pool-exception',
+        title: status === 'approved' ? 'Γ£à Prize-pool exception approved' : 'Γ¥î Prize-pool exception rejected',
+        body: `Your request for Γé╣${Number(record.requestedAmount).toLocaleString('en-IN')} was ${status}. ${note || ''}`.trim(),
+      })
+      .catch(() => undefined);
+    return saved;
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * Applies the reputation transition rules from the platform policy.
+   * Only recorded facts are evaluated ΓÇö an unverified report never changes a level.
+   */
+  async evaluate(username: string, facts: ReputationFacts, reviewer: string) {
+    const result = await this.dataSource.transaction(async (manager) => {
+      const repo = manager.getRepository(OrganiserReputationEntity);
+      let profile = await repo.findOne({ where: { username }, lock: { mode: 'pessimistic_write' } });
+      if (!profile) {
+        profile = await repo.save(repo.create({ username, level: 'blue' }));
+      }
+      this.normalize(profile);
+
+      const qualifying = Number(facts.qualifyingAmount || 0);
+      if (qualifying >= QUALIFYING_THRESHOLDS.blueToGreen) profile.qualifyingAbove50k += 1;
+      if (qualifying >= QUALIFYING_THRESHOLDS.orangeToBlue) profile.qualifyingAbove7k += 1;
+      if (qualifying >= QUALIFYING_THRESHOLDS.redToOrange) profile.qualifyingAbove5k += 1;
+      if (facts.paidOnTime) profile.paidOnTimeCount += 1;
+      profile.overduePaymentCount += Number(facts.overduePayments || 0);
+      profile.mediumViolationCount += Number(facts.mediumViolations || 0);
+      if (facts.outstandingObligations !== undefined) {
+        profile.outstandingObligations = Number(facts.outstandingObligations) || 0;
+      }
+
+      const previous = profile.level;
+      let next: ReputationLevel = profile.level;
+      let policy = '';
+      let reason = facts.reason || 'Reputation evaluation';
+      let sanction: { type: SanctionType; days?: number; reason: string } | null = null;
+
+      const serious = !!facts.seriousMisconduct;
+      const recoveryViolation = !!facts.failedPaymentDuringRecovery || !!facts.involvedInValidDispute;
+      const banActive = profile.bannedUntil ? new Date(profile.bannedUntil).getTime() > Date.now() : false;
+
+      if (profile.lifetimeBan) {
+        // Nothing can lift a lifetime ban automatically.
+        next = profile.level;
+        policy = 'lifetime_ban_active';
+      } else if (profile.level === 'red') {
+        if (serious) {
+          next = 'red';
+          policy = 'serious_misconduct';
+        } else if (banActive) {
+          next = 'red';
+          policy = 'ban_active';
+        } else if (recoveryViolation) {
+          next = 'red';
+          if (profile.banCount > 0) {
+            profile.lifetimeBan = true;
+            policy = 'lifetime_ban_repeat';
+            sanction = { type: 'lifetime_ban', reason: 'Repeat violation after a completed one-month organising ban.' };
+          } else {
+            profile.banCount += 1;
+            profile.bannedUntil = new Date(Date.now() + RED_BAN_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
+            policy = 'red_one_month_ban';
+            sanction = { type: 'one_month_ban', days: RED_BAN_DAYS, reason: 'Failed to pay the prize pool or involved in a valid dispute during Red recovery.' };
+          }
+        } else if (
+          profile.qualifyingAbove5k >= RECOVERY_COMPETITIONS_REQUIRED &&
+          facts.paidOnTime &&
+          profile.mediumViolationCount === 0 &&
+          profile.outstandingObligations <= 0
+        ) {
+          next = 'orange';
+          policy = 'red_to_orange_recovery';
+          profile.qualifyingAbove5k = 0;
+          profile.qualifyingAbove7k = 0;
+          profile.overduePaymentCount = 0;
+          profile.mediumViolationCount = 0;
+        }
+      } else if (serious) {
+        next = 'red';
+        policy = 'serious_misconduct';
+      } else if (profile.level === 'blue') {
+        if (profile.overduePaymentCount >= 2 || profile.mediumViolationCount >= 2) {
+          next = profile.orangeRecoveryCount > 0 ? 'red' : 'orange';
+          policy = profile.orangeRecoveryCount > 0 ? 'repeat_orange_after_recovery' : 'repeated_lower_severity';
+        } else if (
+          profile.qualifyingAbove50k > RECOVERY_COMPETITIONS_REQUIRED &&
+          profile.paidOnTimeCount > 0 &&
+          profile.overduePaymentCount === 0 &&
+          profile.outstandingObligations <= 0
+        ) {
+          next = 'green';
+          policy = 'blue_to_green';
+        }
+      } else if (profile.level === 'green') {
+        if (profile.overduePaymentCount >= 2 || profile.mediumViolationCount >= 2) {
+          next = 'orange';
+          policy = 'green_downgrade';
+          profile.qualifyingAbove7k = 0;
+          profile.qualifyingAbove5k = 0;
+        }
+      } else if (profile.level === 'orange') {
+        if (profile.orangeRecoveryCount > 0 && (profile.overduePaymentCount >= 2 || profile.mediumViolationCount >= 2)) {
+          next = 'red';
+          policy = 'repeat_orange_after_recovery';
+        } else if (profile.overduePaymentCount >= 2 || profile.mediumViolationCount >= 2) {
+          next = 'orange';
+          policy = 'repeated_lower_severity';
+        } else if (
+          profile.qualifyingAbove7k >= RECOVERY_COMPETITIONS_REQUIRED &&
+          facts.paidOnTime &&
+          profile.mediumViolationCount === 0 &&
+          profile.outstandingObligations <= 0
+        ) {
+          next = 'blue';
+          policy = 'orange_to_blue_recovery';
+          profile.orangeRecoveryCount += 1;
+          profile.qualifyingAbove7k = 0;
+          profile.overduePaymentCount = 0;
+          profile.mediumViolationCount = 0;
+        }
+      }
+
+      profile.level = next;
+      await repo.save(profile);
+
+      if (sanction) {
+        await manager.getRepository(OrganiserSanctionEntity).save(
+          manager.getRepository(OrganiserSanctionEntity).create({
+            username,
+            type: sanction.type,
+            reason: sanction.reason,
+            reportId: facts.reportId || null,
+            appliedBy: reviewer,
+            startsAt: new Date(),
+            endsAt: sanction.days ? new Date(Date.now() + sanction.days * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000) : null,
+            active: true,
+          }),
+        );
+      }
+
+      if (next !== previous) {
+        await manager.getRepository(OrganiserReputationHistoryEntity).save(
+          manager.getRepository(OrganiserReputationHistoryEntity).create({
+            username,
+            fromLevel: previous,
+            toLevel: next,
+            reason,
+            policy: policy || null,
+            reportId: facts.reportId || null,
+            reviewer,
+          }),
+        );
+      }
+
+      return { username, previousLevel: previous, level: next, policy, reason, profile };
+    });
+
+    if (result.previousLevel !== result.level) {
+      await this.auditLog.record('ORGANISER_REPUTATION_CHANGED', {
+        actor: reviewer,
+        entityType: 'organiser_reputation',
+        entityId: result.username,
+        details: `@${result.username}: ${result.previousLevel} ΓåÆ ${result.level} (${result.policy || 'policy'}). ${result.reason}`,
+        metadata: { from: result.previousLevel, to: result.level, policy: result.policy, reportId: (facts as any).reportId || null },
+      });
+      await this.notifications
+        .create({
+          toUsername: result.username,
+          type: 'reputation-changed',
+          title: `Organiser reputation: ${result.level.toUpperCase()}`,
+          body: `Your organiser reputation changed from ${result.previousLevel} to ${result.level}. ${result.reason}`,
+        })
+        .catch(() => undefined);
+    }
+
+    return {
+      username: result.username,
+      previousLevel: result.previousLevel,
+      level: result.level,
+      policy: result.policy,
+      limit: this.effectiveLimit(result.level, await this.getGlobalMax()),
+    };
+  }
+
+  /** Direct downgrade recorded by a community moderator decision. */
+  async applyDowngrade(
+    username: string,
+    targetLevel: ReputationLevel,
+    reason: string,
+    reportId: string | null,
+    reviewer: string,
+  ) {
+    if (!isReputationLevel(targetLevel)) throw new BadRequestException('Unknown reputation level.');
+    const profile = await this.getOrCreate(username);
+    if (levelRank(targetLevel) <= levelRank(profile.level)) {
+      throw new BadRequestException(
+        `A downgrade must move the organiser to a lower level (current: ${profile.level}, requested: ${targetLevel}).`,
+      );
+    }
+    const previous = profile.level;
+    profile.level = targetLevel;
+    if (targetLevel === 'red') profile.banCount = profile.banCount;
+    await this.reputation.save(profile);
+    await this.history.save(
+      this.history.create({ username, fromLevel: previous, toLevel: targetLevel, reason, policy: 'moderator_decision', reportId, reviewer }),
+    );
+    await this.auditLog.record('ORGANISER_REPUTATION_DOWNGRADED', {
+      actor: reviewer,
+      entityType: 'organiser_reputation',
+      entityId: username,
+      details: `@${username}: ${previous} ΓåÆ ${targetLevel}. ${reason}`,
+      metadata: { reportId },
+    });
+    await this.notifications
+      .create({
+        toUsername: username,
+        type: 'reputation-changed',
+        title: `Organiser reputation downgraded to ${targetLevel.toUpperCase()}`,
+        body: reason,
+      })
+      .catch(() => undefined);
+    return this.getProfile(username);
+  }
+
+  /** Records a sanction and enforces its effects (ban window / lifetime ban). */
+  async issueSanction(
+    username: string,
+    type: SanctionType,
+    reason: string,
+    options: { reportId?: string | null; days?: number; appliedBy: string },
+  ) {
+    if (!reason || reason.trim().length < 5) {
+      throw new BadRequestException('A sanction reason is required.');
+    }
+    const profile = await this.getOrCreate(username);
+    const endsAt = options.days ? new Date(Date.now() + options.days * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000) : null;
+    const sanction = await this.sanctions.save(
+      this.sanctions.create({
+        username,
+        type,
+        reason: reason.trim(),
+        reportId: options.reportId || null,
+        appliedBy: options.appliedBy,
+        startsAt: new Date(),
+        endsAt,
+        active: true,
+      }),
+    );
+    if (type === 'one_month_ban') {
+      profile.bannedUntil = endsAt;
+      profile.banCount += 1;
+      await this.reputation.save(profile);
+    }
+    if (type === 'lifetime_ban') {
+      profile.lifetimeBan = true;
+      await this.reputation.save(profile);
+    }
+    if (['formal_warning', 'final_warning'].includes(type)) {
+      await this.notifications
+        .create({
+          toUsername: username,
+          type: `organiser-${type}`,
+          title: type === 'final_warning' ? 'Final warning (organiser)' : 'Formal warning (organiser)',
+          body: reason,
+        })
+        .catch(() => undefined);
+    }
+    await this.auditLog.record('ORGANISER_SANCTION_APPLIED', {
+      actor: options.appliedBy,
+      entityType: 'organiser_sanction',
+      entityId: sanction.id,
+      details: `${type} applied to @${username}. ${reason}`,
+      metadata: { reportId: options.reportId || null, days: options.days || null },
+    });
+    return sanction;
+  }
+
+  async getHistory(username: string) {
+    const [reputationHistory, sanctionRows] = await Promise.all([
+      this.history.find({ where: { username }, order: { createdAt: 'DESC' } }),
+      this.sanctions.find({ where: { username }, order: { createdAt: 'DESC' } }),
+    ]);
+    return { reputationHistory, sanctions: sanctionRows };
+  }
+}

+/**
+ * NEXUS ESPORTS ΓÇö organiser reputation, prize-pool enforcement and reporting rules.
+ *
+ * Run with: npm test (backend)
+ *
+ * Service-level tests with in-memory repository fakes, so the real transition
+ * rules and the backend prize-pool gate are exercised without PostgreSQL.
+ */
+import 'reflect-metadata';
+import assert = require('node:assert/strict');
+import { BadRequestException } from '@nestjs/common';
+import { ReputationService } from '../src/modules/reputation/reputation.service';
+import { OrganiserReputationEntity } from '../src/entities/organiser-reputation.entity';
+import { OrganiserReputationHistoryEntity } from '../src/entities/organiser-reputation-history.entity';
+import { OrganiserSanctionEntity } from '../src/entities/organiser-sanction.entity';
+import { PrizePoolExceptionEntity } from '../src/entities/prize-pool-exception.entity';
+import { PlatformSettingEntity } from '../src/entities/platform-setting.entity';
+import {
+  MAX_DISCARDED_REPORTS,
+  REPORT_REASONS,
+  REPUTATION_LEVEL_LIMITS,
+} from '../src/common/reporting';
+import { MAX_PRIZE_POOL } from '../src/common/validation/competition.rules';
+
+let passed = 0;
+let failed = 0;
+
+async function test(name: string, fn: () => Promise<void> | void) {
+  try {
+    await fn();
+    passed += 1;
+    console.log(`  PASS  ${name}`);
+  } catch (err: any) {
+    failed += 1;
+    console.log(`  FAIL  ${name}`);
+    console.log(`        ${err?.message || err}`);
+  }
+}
+
+const isNullOperator = (value: any) => value && typeof value === 'object' && value.type === 'isNull';
+
+const matches = (row: any, where: any) =>
+  Object.entries(where || {}).every(([key, expected]: [string, any]) => {
+    if (isNullOperator(expected)) return row[key] === null || row[key] === undefined;
+    return row[key] === expected;
+  });
+
+class FakeRepo {
+  rows: any[] = [];
+  private seq = 1;
+
+  create(data: any) {
+    return { ...data };
+  }
+
+  private keyOf(row: any) {
+    return row.id !== undefined ? 'id' : 'username';
+  }
+
+  async save(entity: any) {
+    const row = { ...entity };
+    const key = this.keyOf(row);
+    if (row.id === undefined) row.id = `generated-${this.seq++}`;
+    const index = this.rows.findIndex((candidate) => candidate[key] === row[key]);
+    if (index >= 0) {
+      this.rows[index] = { ...this.rows[index], ...row };
+      return this.rows[index];
+    }
+    this.rows.push(row);
+    return row;
+  }
+
+  async findOne(options: any) {
+    return this.rows.find((row) => matches(row, options?.where)) || null;
+  }
+
+  async find(options: any = {}) {
+    const where = options.where;
+    let rows = Array.isArray(where)
+      ? this.rows.filter((row) => where.some((clause) => matches(row, clause)))
+      : this.rows.filter((row) => matches(row, where));
+    if (options.take) rows = rows.slice(0, options.take);
+    return rows;
+  }
+
+  async upsert(entity: any) {
+    const key = this.keyOf(entity);
+    const index = this.rows.findIndex((row) => row[key] === entity[key]);
+    if (index >= 0) this.rows[index] = { ...this.rows[index], ...entity };
+    else this.rows.push({ ...entity });
+    return entity;
+  }
+
+  async count(options: any) {
+    return (await this.find(options)).length;
+  }
+
+  createQueryBuilder() {
+    const repo = this;
+    return {
+      update: () => ({
+        set: (patch: any) => ({
+          where: (sql: string, params: any) => ({
+            execute: async () => {
+              const row = repo.rows.find(
+                (candidate) =>
+                  candidate.id === params.id &&
+                  candidate.status === params.status &&
+                  (candidate.competitionId === null || candidate.competitionId === undefined) &&
+                  Number(candidate.requestedAmount) >= Number(params.amount),
+              );
+              if (!row) return { affected: 0 };
+              Object.assign(row, patch);
+              return { affected: 1 };
+            },
+          }),
+        }),
+      }),
+    };
+  }
+}
+
+function makeService() {
+  // One repository fake per entity, shared by the service and by the
+  // transaction manager (exactly like a real DataSource + EntityManager).
+  const rep = new FakeRepo();
+  const history = new FakeRepo();
+  const sanctions = new FakeRepo();
+  const exceptions = new FakeRepo();
+  const settings = new FakeRepo();
+  const repositories = new Map<any, FakeRepo>([
+    [OrganiserReputationEntity, rep],
+    [OrganiserReputationHistoryEntity, history],
+    [OrganiserSanctionEntity, sanctions],
+    [PrizePoolExceptionEntity, exceptions],
+    [PlatformSettingEntity, settings],
+  ]);
+  const repoFor = (entity: any) => {
+    if (!repositories.has(entity)) repositories.set(entity, new FakeRepo());
+    return repositories.get(entity)!;
+  };
+  const manager = { getRepository: (entity: any) => repoFor(entity) };
+  const dataSource = { transaction: async (fn: any) => fn(manager) };
+  const notifications = { create: async (..._args: any[]) => undefined as any };
+  const auditLog = { record: async () => undefined };
+  const service = new ReputationService(
+    rep as any,
+    history as any,
+    sanctions as any,
+    exceptions as any,
+    settings as any,
+    dataSource as any,
+    notifications as any,
+    auditLog as any,
+  );
+  return { service, rep, settings, exceptions, sanctions };
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  console.log('\nOrganiser reputation levels');
+  const { service, rep, settings, exceptions, sanctions } = makeService();
+
+  await test('new organisers start Blue with the Γé╣3,00,000 platform ceiling', async () => {
+    const profile = await service.getProfile('neworganiser');
+    assert.equal(profile.level, 'blue');
+    assert.equal(profile.levelLimit, 300000);
+    assert.equal(profile.limit, 300000);
+    assert.equal(MAX_PRIZE_POOL, 300000);
+    assert.equal(REPUTATION_LEVEL_LIMITS.orange, 20000);
+    assert.equal(REPUTATION_LEVEL_LIMITS.red, 10000);
+  });
+
+  await test('Blue ΓåÆ Green needs more than 3 qualifying Γé╣50k+ tournaments with on-time payments', async () => {
+    await service.evaluate('grower', { qualifyingAmount: 50000, paidOnTime: true }, 'communitymod');
+    await service.evaluate('grower', { qualifyingAmount: 50000, paidOnTime: true }, 'communitymod');
+    await service.evaluate('grower', { qualifyingAmount: 50000, paidOnTime: true }, 'communitymod');
+    assert.equal((await service.getProfile('grower')).level, 'blue');
+    await service.evaluate('grower', { qualifyingAmount: 50000, paidOnTime: true }, 'communitymod');
+    assert.equal((await service.getProfile('grower')).level, 'green');
+  });
+
+  await test('Blue ΓåÆ Orange requires repeated substantiated lower-severity issues', async () => {
+    await service.evaluate('slipping', { overduePayments: 1 }, 'communitymod');
+    assert.equal((await service.getProfile('slipping')).level, 'blue');
+    await service.evaluate('slipping', { overduePayments: 1 }, 'communitymod');
+    const profile = await service.getProfile('slipping');
+    assert.equal(profile.level, 'orange');
+    assert.equal(profile.limit, 20000);
+  });
+
+  await test('Blue ΓåÆ Red for substantiated serious misconduct', async () => {
+    const result = await service.evaluate('fraudster', { seriousMisconduct: true, reason: 'Deliberate prize non-payment confirmed.' }, 'communitymod');
+    assert.equal(result.level, 'red');
+    assert.equal((await service.getProfile('fraudster')).limit, 10000);
+  });
+
+  await test('Orange ΓåÆ Blue recovery, then a second Orange escalates to Red', async () => {
+    const record = await rep.save(rep.create({ username: 'repeat', level: 'orange', orangeRecoveryCount: 0 }));
+    assert.ok(record.username);
+    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i += 1) {
+      await service.evaluate('repeat', { qualifyingAmount: 7000, paidOnTime: true }, 'communitymod');
+    }
+    const recovered = await service.getProfile('repeat');
+    assert.equal(recovered.level, 'blue');
+    assert.equal(recovered.counters.orangeRecoveryCount, 1);
+
+    await service.evaluate('repeat', { overduePayments: 2 }, 'communitymod');
+    assert.equal((await service.getProfile('repeat')).level, 'red');
+  });
+
+  await test('Red ΓåÆ Orange recovery after 3 additional Γé╣5k+ tournaments', async () => {
+    await rep.save(rep.create({ username: 'recovering', level: 'red' }));
+    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i += 1) {
+      await service.evaluate('recovering', { qualifyingAmount: 5000, paidOnTime: true }, 'communitymod');
+    }
+    assert.equal((await service.getProfile('recovering')).level, 'orange');
+  });
+
+  await test('Red non-payment triggers a one-month ban, then a lifetime ban on repeat', async () => {
+    await rep.save(rep.create({ username: 'banned', level: 'red' }));
+    const first = await service.evaluate('banned', { failedPaymentDuringRecovery: true }, 'communitymod');
+    assert.equal(first.level, 'red');
+    let profile = await service.getProfile('banned');
+    assert.equal(profile.banCount, 1);
+    assert.ok(profile.bannedUntil && new Date(profile.bannedUntil).getTime() > Date.now());
+    assert.ok(sanctions.rows.some((row: any) => row.username === 'banned' && row.type === 'one_month_ban'));
+
+    // Ban completed, then another qualifying failure in the recovery window.
+    const row = await rep.findOne({ where: { username: 'banned' } });
+    row.bannedUntil = new Date(Date.now() - 1000);
+    await rep.save(row);
+    await service.evaluate('banned', { failedPaymentDuringRecovery: true }, 'communitymod');
+    profile = await service.getProfile('banned');
+    assert.equal(profile.lifetimeBan, true);
+    assert.ok(sanctions.rows.some((row: any) => row.username === 'banned' && row.type === 'lifetime_ban'));
+    await assert.rejects(() => service.assertCanOrganise('banned'), /permanently banned/i);
+  });
+
+  await test('Green downgrades for repeated medium-severity violations', async () => {
+    await rep.save(rep.create({ username: 'greeny', level: 'green' }));
+    await service.evaluate('greeny', { mediumViolations: 2 }, 'communitymod');
+    assert.equal((await service.getProfile('greeny')).level, 'orange');
+  });
+
+  console.log('\nPrize-pool enforcement');
+
+  await test('Blue and Green are limited by the Γé╣3,00,000 ceiling, Orange and Red by their own limit', async () => {
+    assert.equal((await service.authorizePrizePool(300000, 'neworganiser')).limit, 300000);
+    await service.evaluate('orangeguy', { overduePayments: 2 }, 'communitymod');
+    await assert.rejects(() => service.authorizePrizePool(50000, 'orangeguy'), /Γé╣20,000/);
+    await assert.rejects(() => service.authorizePrizePool(10001, 'fraudster'), /Γé╣10,000/);
+    assert.equal((await service.authorizePrizePool(10000, 'fraudster')).limit, 10000);
+  });
+
+  await test('No organiser can exceed the platform ceiling, even with an approved exception', async () => {
+    await assert.rejects(() => service.authorizePrizePool(300001, 'neworganiser'), /platform-wide maximum/i);
+    await assert.rejects(() => service.requestException('neworganiser', 300001, 'Test exception above the ceiling.'), /never exceed/i);
+  });
+
+  await test('A configurable ceiling is enforced centrally', async () => {
+    await service.setGlobalMax(100000, 'superadmin1');
+    assert.equal(await service.getGlobalMax(), 100000);
+    await assert.rejects(() => service.authorizePrizePool(150000, 'neworganiser'), /platform-wide maximum/i);
+    await service.setGlobalMax(300000, 'superadmin1');
+  });
+
+  await test('An approved exception applies to one competition and disappears when consumed', async () => {
+    const request = await service.requestException('orangeguy', 100000, 'Sponsor-backed event above the Orange limit.');
+    await service.reviewException(request.id, 'approved', 'Approved for one event.', 'compadmin');
+    const gate = await service.authorizePrizePool(50000, 'orangeguy');
+    assert.equal(gate.exceptionId, request.id);
+    await service.consumeException(request.id, 'competition-1', 50000);
+    await assert.rejects(() => service.consumeException(request.id, 'competition-2', 50000), /no longer available/i);
+    // The permanent limit and reputation are untouched by the exception.
+    const profile = await service.getProfile('orangeguy');
+    assert.equal(profile.level, 'orange');
+    assert.equal(profile.limit, 20000);
+    assert.equal(exceptions.rows.find((row: any) => row.id === request.id).competitionId, 'competition-1');
+    // Without a fresh exception the Orange limit still applies.
+    await assert.rejects(() => service.authorizePrizePool(50000, 'orangeguy'), /Γé╣20,000/);
+  });
+
+  await test('Reputation and sanction history is written for every change', async () => {
+    const history = await service.getHistory('fraudster');
+    assert.ok(history.reputationHistory.length >= 1);
+    assert.equal(history.reputationHistory[0].toLevel, 'red');
+    assert.ok(history.reputationHistory[0].reviewer);
+    assert.ok(history.reputationHistory[0].reason);
+  });
+
+  console.log('\nReporting policy constants');
+
+  await test('priority follows the backend reason catalogue, never the UI', () => {
+    assert.equal(REPORT_REASONS.cheating.priority, 'high');
+    assert.equal(REPORT_REASONS.harassment.priority, 'medium');
+    assert.equal(REPORT_REASONS.offensive_name.priority, 'low');
+    assert.equal(REPORT_REASONS.prize_not_received.category, 'financial');
+    assert.equal(MAX_DISCARDED_REPORTS, 3);
+    assert.equal(settings.rows.length, 1); // only the platform ceiling row
+  });
+
+  console.log(`\n${passed} passed, ${failed} failed`);
+  if (failed) process.exitCode = 1;
+}
+
+main().catch((err) => {
+  console.error(err);
+  process.exit(1);
+});

+/**
+ * NEXUS ESPORTS ΓÇö organiser reputation (self-fetching)
+ * Rendered in competition information so the organiser's level is visible
+ * wherever the competition is shown.
+ */
+import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
+import { LocalReputationAPI as ReputationAPI } from '../services/local/reporting.js';
+import ReputationBadge from './ReputationBadge.jsx';
+
+export default function OrganiserReputation({ username, showLimit = true }) {
+  const [profile, setProfile] = useState(null);
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    let alive = true;
+    if (!username) return () => { alive = false; };
+    ReputationAPI.profile(username)
+      .then((res) => { if (alive && res.ok && res.data) setProfile(res.data); })
+      .catch(() => undefined);
+    return () => { alive = false; };
+  }, [username]);
+
+  if (!profile) return null;
+  return <ReputationBadge level={profile.level} limit={profile.limit} showLimit={showLimit} />;
+}

+/**
+ * NEXUS ESPORTS ΓÇö report decision modals
+ *
+ * Discard, organiser decision (against a player) and Community Moderator
+ * decision (against an organiser). All decisions are sent to the backend, which
+ * re-validates permissions, findings and actions.
+ */
+import { useState } from 'react';
+import {
+  DISCARD_REASONS,
+  FINDINGS,
+  LEVEL_META,
+  MODERATOR_ACTIONS,
+  ORGANISER_ACTIONS,
+} from '../lib/reporting.js';
+import { LocalReportsAPI as ReportsAPI, LocalReputationAPI as ReputationAPI } from '../services/local/reporting.js';
+
+function Backdrop({ children, onClose }) {
+  return (
+    <div className="report-modal-backdrop" onClick={(event) => { if (event.target === event.currentTarget) onClose(); }}>
+      <div className="report-modal">{children}</div>
+    </div>
+  );
+}
+
+export function DiscardModal({ report, onClose, onDone }) {
+  const [reason, setReason] = useState('duplicate_report');
+  const [note, setNote] = useState('');
+  const [error, setError] = useState('');
+  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
+
+  async function submit() {
+    setError('');
+    if (reason === 'other' && note.trim().length < 10) {
+      setError('Explain the reason for discarding this report (at least 10 characters).');
+      return;
+    }
+    setBusy(true);
+    const res = await ReportsAPI.discard(report.id, reason, note.trim());
+    setBusy(false);
+    if (!res.ok) { setError(res.error || 'Unable to discard this report.'); return; }
+    onDone && onDone(res.data);
+  }
+
+  return (
+    <Backdrop onClose={onClose}>
+      <h2>Discard report</h2>
+      <p className="sub">A discarded report counts towards the reporter's three baseless reports for this competition.</p>
+      {error && <div className="report-inline-error">{error}</div>}
+      <div className="report-field">
+        <label htmlFor="discard-reason">Mandatory reason</label>
+        <select id="discard-reason" value={reason} onChange={(e) => setReason(e.target.value)}>
+          {DISCARD_REASONS.map((option) => (
+            <option key={option.value} value={option.value}>{option.label}</option>
+          ))}
+        </select>
+      </div>
+      <div className="report-field">
+        <label htmlFor="discard-note">
+          Notes
+          {reason === 'other' ? <span className="hint">Required</span> : <span className="hint">Optional</span>}
+        </label>
+        <textarea id="discard-note" rows={3} value={note} maxLength={500} onChange={(e) => setNote(e.target.value)} />
+      </div>
+      <div className="report-modal-actions">
+        <button className="report-btn ghost" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</button>
+        <button className="report-btn danger" disabled={busy} onClick={submit}>{busy ? 'DiscardingΓÇª' : 'Discard report'}</button>
+      </div>
+    </Backdrop>
+  );
+}
+
+export function PlayerDecisionModal({ report, onClose, onDone }) {
+  const [finding, setFinding] = useState('substantiated');
+  const [action, setAction] = useState('formal_warning');
+  const [conclusion, setConclusion] = useState('');
+  const [error, setError] = useState('');
+  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
+
+  async function submit() {
+    setError('');
+    if (conclusion.trim().length < 15) {
+      setError('Write a conclusion explaining what happened and why this decision was reached.');
+      return;
+    }
+    setBusy(true);
+    const res = await ReportsAPI.actPlayer(report.id, { finding, action, conclusion: conclusion.trim() });
+    setBusy(false);
+    if (!res.ok) { setError(res.error || 'Unable to record this decision.'); return; }
+    onDone && onDone(res.data);
+  }
+
+  return (
+    <Backdrop onClose={onClose}>
+      <h2>Record decision</h2>
+      <p className="sub">{report.code || report.id} ┬╖ against @{report.reportedUsername}</p>
+      {error && <div className="report-inline-error">{error}</div>}
+      <div className="report-field">
+        <label htmlFor="finding">Finding</label>
+        <select id="finding" value={finding} onChange={(e) => setFinding(e.target.value)}>
+          {FINDINGS.map((option) => <option key={option.value} value={option.value}>{option.label}</option>)}
+        </select>
+      </div>
+      <div className="report-field">
+        <label htmlFor="action">Action</label>
+        <select id="action" value={action} onChange={(e) => setAction(e.target.value)}>
+          {ORGANISER_ACTIONS.map((option) => <option key={option.value} value={option.value}>{option.label}</option>)}
+        </select>
+        <span className="hint">
+          Removal transfers team leadership to the next active, eligible player. A warning adds to the
+          player's confirmed warning count.
+        </span>
+      </div>
+      <div className="report-field">
+        <label htmlFor="conclusion">Conclusion (required)</label>
+        <textarea id="conclusion" rows={4} maxLength={2000} value={conclusion} onChange={(e) => setConclusion(e.target.value)} />
+      </div>
+      <div className="report-modal-actions">
+        <button className="report-btn ghost" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</button>
+        <button className="report-btn primary" disabled={busy} onClick={submit}>{busy ? 'SavingΓÇª' : 'Confirm action'}</button>
+      </div>
+    </Backdrop>
+  );
+}
+
+export function ModeratorDecisionModal({ report, onClose, onDone }) {
+  const [finding, setFinding] = useState('substantiated');
+  const [action, setAction] = useState('formal_warning');
+  const [conclusion, setConclusion] = useState('');
+  const [level, setLevel] = useState('orange');
+  const [days, setDays] = useState(30);
+  const [error, setError] = useState('');
+  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
+  const needsLevel = action === 'reputation_downgrade';
+  const needsDays = action === 'creation_restriction' || action === 'competition_suspension';
+
+  async function submit() {
+    setError('');
+    if (conclusion.trim().length < 15) {
+      setError('A recorded conclusion explaining the decision is required.');
+      return;
+    }
+    if (finding === 'unsubstantiated' && !['no_action', 'formal_warning'].includes(action)) {
+      setError('An unsubstantiated finding cannot justify a punitive action.');
+      return;
+    }
+    setBusy(true);
+    const payload = { finding, action, conclusion: conclusion.trim() };
+    if (needsLevel) payload.level = level;
+    if (needsDays) payload.days = Number(days) || 30;
+    const res = await ReportsAPI.actOrganiser(report.id, payload);
+    setBusy(false);
+    if (!res.ok) { setError(res.error || 'Unable to record this decision.'); return; }
+    onDone && onDone(res.data);
+  }
+
+  return (
+    <Backdrop onClose={onClose}>
+      <h2>Decision against organiser</h2>
+      <p className="sub">{report.code || report.id} ┬╖ @{report.reportedUsername}</p>
+      {error && <div className="report-inline-error">{error}</div>}
+      <div className="report-field">
+        <label htmlFor="m-finding">Finding</label>
+        <select id="m-finding" value={finding} onChange={(e) => setFinding(e.target.value)}>
+          {FINDINGS.map((option) => <option key={option.value} value={option.value}>{option.label}</option>)}
+        </select>
+      </div>
+      <div className="report-field">
+        <label htmlFor="m-action">Action</label>
+        <select id="m-action" value={action} onChange={(e) => setAction(e.target.value)}>
+          {MODERATOR_ACTIONS.map((option) => <option key={option.value} value={option.value}>{option.label}</option>)}
+        </select>
+        <span className="hint">A warning never changes reputation by itself. Downgrades are audited with the reason.</span>
+      </div>
+      {needsLevel && (
+        <div className="report-field">
+          <label htmlFor="m-level">New reputation level</label>
+          <select id="m-level" value={level} onChange={(e) => setLevel(e.target.value)}>
+            {Object.entries(LEVEL_META).map(([key, meta]) => (
+              <option key={key} value={key}>{meta.label} ΓÇö max Γé╣{meta.limit.toLocaleString('en-IN')}</option>
+            ))}
+          </select>
+        </div>
+      )}
+      {needsDays && (
+        <div className="report-field">
+          <label htmlFor="m-days">Duration (days)</label>
+          <input id="m-days" type="number" min={1} max={365} value={days} onChange={(e) => setDays(e.target.value)} />
+        </div>
+      )}
+      <div className="report-field">
+        <label htmlFor="m-conclusion">Conclusion (required)</label>
+        <textarea id="m-conclusion" rows={4} maxLength={2000} value={conclusion} onChange={(e) => setConclusion(e.target.value)} />
+      </div>
+      <div className="report-modal-actions">
+        <button className="report-btn ghost" onClick={onClose}>Cancel</button>
+        <button className="report-btn primary" disabled={busy} onClick={submit}>{busy ? 'SavingΓÇª' : 'Confirm action'}</button>
+      </div>
+    </Backdrop>
+  );
+}
+
+export async function changePriority(reportId, priority) {
+  return ReportsAPI.priority(reportId, priority);
+}
+
+export async function requestPrizeException(amount, reason) {
+  return ReputationAPI.requestException(amount, reason);
+}
diff --git a/frontend/src/components/ReportButton.jsx b/frontend/src/components/ReportButton.jsx
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..1050a5a

+++ b/frontend/src/components/ReportButton.jsx
@@ -0,0 +1,78 @@
+/**
+ * NEXUS ESPORTS ΓÇö Report button
+ *
+ * Small inline action placed beside eligible player / team-leader /
+ * organiser names. It links to the context-aware report form; the backend is
+ * still the authority for eligibility and the three-discard restriction.
+ */
+import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
+import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
+import { LocalReportsAPI as ReportsAPI } from '../services/local/reporting.js';
+import { subscribe } from '../services/local/storage.js';
+
+export function reportHref({ compId, against, targetRole = 'player', matchId, teamId, source = 'page' }) {
+  const params = new URLSearchParams();
+  if (compId) params.set('compId', compId);
+  if (against) params.set('against', against);
+  if (targetRole) params.set('role', targetRole);
+  if (matchId) params.set('matchId', matchId);
+  if (teamId) params.set('teamId', teamId);
+  if (source) params.set('source', source);
+  return `/pages/report.html?${params.toString()}`;
+}
+
+export default function ReportButton({
+  compId,
+  against,
+  targetRole = 'player',
+  matchId,
+  teamId,
+  source,
+  label = 'Report',
+  className = 'report-btn',
+}) {
+  const [restricted, setRestricted] = useState(false);
+  const [checking, setChecking] = useState(true);
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    let alive = true;
+    if (!compId) {
+      setChecking(false);
+      return () => { alive = false; };
+    }
+    const check = () => {
+      ReportsAPI.restrictions(compId)
+        .then((res) => {
+          if (!alive) return;
+          if (res?.ok && res.data) setRestricted(!!res.data.restricted);
+        })
+        .catch(() => undefined)
+        .finally(() => { if (alive) setChecking(false); });
+    };
+    check();
+    const unsubscribe = subscribe(check);
+    return () => { alive = false; unsubscribe(); };
+  }, [compId]);
+
+  if (restricted) {
+    return (
+      <span
+        className="report-btn ghost"
+        title="You reached the limit of 3 baseless reports in this competition."
+        style={{ opacity: 0.5, cursor: 'not-allowed' }}
+      >
+        Reporting locked
+      </span>
+    );
+  }
+
+  return (
+    <Link
+      className={className}
+      to={reportHref({ compId, against, targetRole, matchId, teamId, source })}
+      title={checking ? 'Checking reporting eligibilityΓÇª' : `Report @${against}`}
+    >
+      {label}
+    </Link>
+  );
+}

+++ b/frontend/src/hooks/useReportSync.js
@@ -0,0 +1,16 @@
+/**
+ * NEXUS ESPORTS ΓÇö report-system sync hook.
+ *
+ * Re-runs the caller's loader whenever this tab or another tab writes to the
+ * local report/reputation store. Pass a stable loader (useCallback) so the
+ * subscription is not recreated on every render.
+ */
+import { useEffect } from 'react';
+import { subscribe } from '../services/local/storage.js';
+
+export default function useReportSync(handler) {
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (typeof handler !== 'function') return undefined;
+    return subscribe(() => handler());
+  }, [handler]);
+}

+    role: 'admin',
+    adminType: 'admin',
+    label: 'Admin',
+    icon: '≡ƒöº',
+    color: '#94a3b8',
+    bg: 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.15)',
+    border: 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.3)',
+  },
+];
+
+const normalize = (value) => String(value || '').trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[\s-]+/g, '_');
+
+/** Match an account's role/adminType to its catalogue entry. */
+export function adminRoleMeta(role, adminType) {
+  const r = normalize(role);
+  const t = normalize(adminType);
+  return ADMIN_ROLE_OPTIONS.find((option) => (
+    normalize(option.role) === r
+    || normalize(option.value) === r
+    || (t && normalize(option.adminType) === t)
+  )) || null;
+}
+
+export function isAdministrativeAccount(account) {
+  if (!account) return false;
+  const r = normalize(account.role);
+  const t = normalize(account.adminType);
+  if (['participant', 'regular', 'team_lead', 'user'].includes(r)) return false;
+  return !!adminRoleMeta(account.role, account.adminType);
+}
+
+/** Accepts either the catalogue value or the stored role value. */
+export function resolveAdminRole(value) {
+  const key = normalize(value);
+  return ADMIN_ROLE_OPTIONS.find((option) => (
+    normalize(option.value) === key || normalize(option.role) === key
+  )) || null;
+}
+
+export const ADMIN_ROLE_VALUES = ADMIN_ROLE_OPTIONS.map((option) => option.value);
+
+/** Roles a Super Admin may assign from the creation form (never participant). */
+export const CREATABLE_ADMIN_ROLES = ADMIN_ROLE_OPTIONS.filter((option) => option.value !== 'admin');

+/**
+ * NEXUS ESPORTS ΓÇö Case Discussion Room
+ *
+ * A controlled room for one report: reviewer, reporter, accused and any
+ * explicitly added witness. Access is enforced server-side; once ended the room
+ * is read-only and the history is preserved.
+ */
+import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
+import { Link, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
+import { LocalReportsAPI as ReportsAPI, prepareEvidenceFile } from '../services/local/reporting.js';
+import useReportSync from '../hooks/useReportSync.js';
+import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext.jsx';
+import { capitalise, formatDate, reportCode } from '../lib/reporting.js';
+import { showToast } from '../lib/toast.js';
+import '../styles/pages/reports.css';
+
+export default function CaseDiscussion() {
+  const [params] = useSearchParams();
+  const discussionId = params.get('discussionId') || '';
+  const { session } = useAuth();
+  const [data, setData] = useState(null);
+  const [error, setError] = useState('');
+  const [body, setBody] = useState('');
+  const [files, setFiles] = useState([]);
+  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
+  const [witness, setWitness] = useState('');
+
+  const load = useCallback(async () => {
+    if (!discussionId) { setError('No case discussion was selected.'); return; }
+    const res = await ReportsAPI.discussion(discussionId);
+    if (!res.ok) { setError(res.error || 'Unable to open this discussion.'); return; }
+    setData(res.data);
+  }, [discussionId]);
+
+  useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);
+  useReportSync(load);
+
+  async function send(event) {
+    event.preventDefault();
+    if (!body.trim()) return;
+    setBusy(true);
+    const evidence = [];
+    for (const file of files) {
+      try {
+        evidence.push(await prepareEvidenceFile(file));
+      } catch (err) {
+        showToast(err?.message || `${file.name} could not be attached.`, 'error');
+      }
+    }
+    const res = await ReportsAPI.postMessage(discussionId, body.trim(), evidence);
+    setBusy(false);
+    if (!res.ok) { showToast(res.error || 'Unable to send the message.', 'error'); return; }
+    setBody('');
+    setFiles([]);
+    load();
+  }
+
+  async function endDiscussion() {
+    const res = await ReportsAPI.endDiscussion(discussionId);
+    if (!res.ok) { showToast(res.error || 'Unable to end the discussion.', 'error'); return; }
+    showToast('Discussion ended. The room is now read-only.', 'success');
+    load();
+  }
+
+  async function addWitness() {
+    if (!witness.trim()) return;
+    const res = await ReportsAPI.addParticipant(discussionId, witness.trim());
+    if (!res.ok) { showToast(res.error || 'Unable to add this participant.', 'error'); return; }
+    setWitness('');
+    load();
+  }
+
+  if (error) {
+    return (
+      <main className="main-content reports-page">
+        <div className="report-inline-error">{error}</div>
+        <Link className="report-btn" to="/pages/my-reports.html">Back to reports</Link>
+      </main>
+    );
+  }
+
+  if (!data) return <main className="main-content reports-page"><div className="report-empty">Loading case discussionΓÇª</div></main>;
+
+  const { discussion, report, participants, messages, evidence, canModerate, readOnly } = data;
+  const me = String(session?.username || '').toLowerCase();
+  const isParticipant = participants.some((p) => String(p.username).toLowerCase() === me);
+
+  return (
+    <main className="main-content reports-page">
+      <div className="reports-head">
+        <div>
+          <h1 className="reports-title">Case discussion ┬╖ {reportCode(report)}</h1>
+          <p className="reports-subtitle">
+            {capitalise(report.reasonCode)} ┬╖ opened {formatDate(discussion.createdAt)} ┬╖{' '}
+            {readOnly ? 'read-only' : 'open'}
+          </p>
+        </div>
+        <Link className="report-btn" to="/pages/my-reports.html">Reports</Link>
+      </div>
+
+      {readOnly && <div className="report-inline-ok">This discussion has ended. The history is preserved and cannot be modified.</div>}
+
+      <div className="discussion-layout">
+        <section className="discussion-thread">
+          <h2 style={{ fontSize: 15, margin: '0 0 12px', color: '#e2e8f0' }}>Messages</h2>
+          <div className="message-list">
+            {messages.length === 0 && <div className="report-empty">No messages yet.</div>}
+            {messages.map((message) => (
+              <article key={message.id} className={`message ${String(message.authorUsername).toLowerCase() === me ? 'mine' : ''}`}>
+                <div className="who">
+                  @{message.authorUsername}
+                  <span className="when">{formatDate(message.createdAt)}</span>
+                </div>
+                <div className="body">{message.body}</div>
+                {Array.isArray(message.evidenceUrls) && message.evidenceUrls.length > 0 && (
+                  <div className="evidence-list">
+                    {message.evidenceUrls.map((url, index) => (
+                      <a className="evidence-item" key={`${message.id}-${index}`} href={url} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">
+                        Evidence {index + 1}
+                      </a>
+                    ))}
+                  </div>
+                )}
+              </article>
+            ))}
+          </div>
+
+          {!readOnly && isParticipant ? (
+            <form onSubmit={send}>
+              <div className="report-field">
+                <label htmlFor="message">Your message</label>
+                <textarea id="message" rows={3} maxLength={2000} value={body} onChange={(e) => setBody(e.target.value)} />
+              </div>
+              <div className="report-form-grid">
+                <div className="report-field">
+                  <label htmlFor="message-evidence">Additional evidence <span className="hint">Optional images</span></label>
+                  <input
+                    id="message-evidence"
+                    type="file"
+                    accept="image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp"
+                    multiple
+                    onChange={(e) => setFiles(Array.from(e.target.files || []).slice(0, 5))}
+                  />
+                </div>
+              </div>
+              <div className="report-actions">
+                <button type="submit" className="report-btn primary" disabled={busy}>
+                  {busy ? 'SendingΓÇª' : 'Send message'}
+                </button>
+                {canModerate && (
+                  <button type="button" className="report-btn danger" onClick={endDiscussion}>
+                    End discussion
+                  </button>
+                )}
+              </div>
+            </form>
+          ) : (
+            <p style={{ color: '#64748b', fontSize: 12.5 }}>
+              {readOnly ? 'This room is closed.' : 'You can read this case but cannot post in it.'}
+            </p>
+          )}
+        </section>
+
+        <aside className="discussion-side">
+          <div className="side-card">
+            <h3>Case</h3>
+            <ul>
+              <li><span>Report</span><span>{reportCode(report)}</span></li>
+              <li><span>Status</span><span>{capitalise(report.status)}</span></li>
+              <li><span>Priority</span><span>{capitalise(report.priority)}</span></li>
+              <li><span>Reporter</span><span>@{report.reporterUsername}</span></li>
+              <li><span>Accused</span><span>@{report.reportedUsername}</span></li>
+              <li><span>Competition</span><span>{report.competitionName}</span></li>
+            </ul>
+          </div>
+
+          <div className="side-card">
+            <h3>Participants</h3>
+            <ul>
+              {participants.map((person) => (
+                <li key={person.id}>
+                  <span>@{person.username}</span>
+                  <span style={{ color: '#c6ff33' }}>{capitalise(person.role)}</span>
+                </li>
+              ))}
+            </ul>
+            {!readOnly && canModerate && (
+              <div style={{ marginTop: 12 }}>
+                <input
+                  className="form-input"
+                  placeholder="Add a witness username"
+                  value={witness}
+                  onChange={(e) => setWitness(e.target.value)}
+                  style={{ width: '100%', marginBottom: 8 }}
+                />
+                <button className="report-btn" onClick={addWitness}>Add participant</button>
+              </div>
+            )}
+          </div>
+
+          <div className="side-card">
+            <h3>Evidence on the report</h3>
+            {evidence.length === 0 ? (
+              <p style={{ color: '#64748b', fontSize: 12 }}>No files attached.</p>
+            ) : (
+              <div className="evidence-list">
+                {evidence.map((file) => (
+                  <a className="evidence-item" key={file.id} href={file.url} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">
+                    {file.filename || 'Evidence'}
+                  </a>
+                ))}
+              </div>
+            )}
+          </div>
+        </aside>
+      </div>
+    </main>
+  );
+}

diff --git a/frontend/src/pages/MyReports.jsx b/frontend/src/pages/MyReports.jsx
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..b1096fa
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/pages/MyReports.jsx
@@ -0,0 +1,218 @@
+/**
+ * NEXUS ESPORTS ΓÇö Reports
+ *
+ * Player view: reports I filed or that were filed against me, the case
+ * discussions I participate in, and (for organisers) the reputation / prize-pool
+ * exception tools.
+ */
+import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
+import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
+import { LocalReportsAPI as ReportsAPI, LocalReputationAPI as ReputationAPI } from '../services/local/reporting.js';
+import useReportSync from '../hooks/useReportSync.js';
+import { useAuth } from '../context/AuthContext.jsx';
+import ReportCard from '../components/ReportCard.jsx';
+import ReputationBadge from '../components/ReputationBadge.jsx';
+import { STATUS_TABS, formatDate, formatINR, capitalise } from '../lib/reporting.js';
+import { showToast } from '../lib/toast.js';
+import '../styles/pages/reports.css';
+
+export default function MyReports() {
+  const { session } = useAuth();
+  const [tab, setTab] = useState('all');
+  const [reports, setReports] = useState([]);
+  const [discussions, setDiscussions] = useState([]);
+  const [reputation, setReputation] = useState(null);
+  const [exceptions, setExceptions] = useState([]);
+  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
+  const [error, setError] = useState('');
+  const [amount, setAmount] = useState('');
+  const [reason, setReason] = useState('');
+  const [requesting, setRequesting] = useState(false);
+
+  const load = useCallback(async () => {
+    setLoading(true);
+    setError('');
+    try {
+      const [mine, threads, profile, myExceptions] = await Promise.all([
+        ReportsAPI.mine(),
+        ReportsAPI.myDiscussions(),
+        ReputationAPI.me(),
+        ReputationAPI.myExceptions(),
+      ]);
+      if (mine.ok && Array.isArray(mine.data)) setReports(mine.data);
+      else if (!mine.ok) setError(mine.error || 'Unable to load your reports.');
+      if (threads.ok && Array.isArray(threads.data)) setDiscussions(threads.data);
+      if (profile.ok && profile.data) setReputation(profile.data);
+      if (myExceptions.ok && Array.isArray(myExceptions.data)) setExceptions(myExceptions.data);
+    } finally {
+      setLoading(false);
+    }
+  }, []);
+
+  useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);
+  useReportSync(load);
+
+  async function requestException() {
+    const value = Number(String(amount).replace(/[^0-9.]/g, ''));
+    if (!value || value <= 0) { showToast('Enter the prize-pool amount you need.', 'error'); return; }
+    if (reason.trim().length < 10) { showToast('Explain why the exception is needed (10+ characters).', 'error'); return; }
+    setRequesting(true);
+    const res = await ReputationAPI.requestException(value, reason.trim());
+    setRequesting(false);
+    if (!res.ok) { showToast(res.error || 'Unable to request an exception.', 'error'); return; }
+    showToast('Exception request submitted for administrative review.', 'success');
+    setAmount('');
+    setReason('');
+    load();
+  }
+
+  const filtered = tab === 'all' ? reports : reports.filter((report) => report.status === tab);
+
+  return (
+    <main className="main-content reports-page">
+      <div className="reports-head">
+        <div>
+          <h1 className="reports-title">Reports</h1>

+                  value={reason}

+                />

+            <button className="report-btn primary" disabled={requesting} onClick={requestException}>
+              {requesting ? 'SubmittingΓÇª' : 'Request exception'}
+            </button>
+
+            {exceptions.length > 0 && (
+              <ul style={{ listStyle: 'none', margin: '14px 0 0', padding: 0, display: 'grid', gap: 7 }}>

+                  <li key={row.id} style={{ fontSize: 12.5, color: '#cbd5e1' }}>
+                    {formatINR(row.requestedAmount)} ┬╖ <strong>{capitalise(row.status)}</strong> ┬╖ {formatDate(row.createdAt)}

+            <div className="report-card" key={thread.discussionId}>

+/**
+ * NEXUS ESPORTS ΓÇö Organiser report details
+ *
+ * Full report file with status history, evidence and the same Discard /
+ * Discuss / Act actions as the queue.
+ */
+import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
+import { Link, useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
+import { LocalReportsAPI as ReportsAPI } from '../services/local/reporting.js';
+import useReportSync from '../hooks/useReportSync.js';
+import { DiscardModal, PlayerDecisionModal } from '../components/ReportActionModals.jsx';
+import { PRIORITY_META, capitalise, formatDate, reportCode } from '../lib/reporting.js';
+import { showToast } from '../lib/toast.js';
+import '../styles/pages/reports.css';
+
+export default function OrganizerReportDetail() {
+  const [params] = useSearchParams();
+  const id = params.get('id') || '';
+  const navigate = useNavigate();
+  const [report, setReport] = useState(null);
+  const [error, setError] = useState('');
+  const [modal, setModal] = useState(null);
+
+  const load = useCallback(async () => {
+    if (!id) { setError('No report selected.'); return; }
+    const res = await ReportsAPI.get(id);
+    if (!res.ok) { setError(res.error || 'Unable to load this report.'); return; }
+    setReport(res.data);
+  }, [id]);
+
+  useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);
+  useReportSync(load);
+
+  async function discuss() {
+    const res = await ReportsAPI.startDiscussion(id);
+    if (!res.ok) { showToast(res.error || 'Unable to open the case discussion.', 'error'); return; }
+    navigate(`/pages/case-discussion.html?discussionId=${res.data.discussion.id}`);
+  }
+
+  if (error) {
+    return (
+      <main className="main-content reports-page">
+        <div className="report-inline-error">{error}</div>
+        <Link className="report-btn" to="/pages/organizer-reports.html">Back to reports</Link>
+      </main>
+    );
+  }
+  if (!report) return <main className="main-content reports-page"><div className="report-empty">Loading reportΓÇª</div></main>;
+
+  const priority = PRIORITY_META[report.priority] || PRIORITY_META.medium;
+  const open = ['new', 'under_review', 'awaiting_response'].includes(report.status);
+
+  return (
+    <main className="main-content reports-page">
+      <div className="reports-head">
+        <div>
+          <h1 className="reports-title">{reportCode(report)}</h1>
+          <p className="reports-subtitle">
+            {capitalise(report.reasonCode)} ┬╖ @{report.reporterUsername} reported @{report.reportedUsername}
+          </p>
+        </div>
+        <Link className="report-btn" to="/pages/organizer-reports.html">Back to reports</Link>
+      </div>
+
+      <div className="report-card">
+        <div className="report-card-top">
+          <span className="report-pill" style={{ color: priority.color, background: priority.bg, border: `1px solid ${priority.border}` }}>
+            {priority.label}
+          </span>
+          <span className="report-pill" style={{ color: '#cbd5e1', border: '1px solid #334155' }}>
+            {capitalise(report.status)}
+          </span>
+          <span className="report-pill" style={{ color: '#94a3b8', border: '1px solid #334155' }}>
+            {capitalise(report.reportedRole)}
+          </span>
+          {report.relatedReportId && (
+            <span className="report-pill" style={{ color: '#60a5fa', border: '1px solid rgba(96,165,250,0.4)' }}>
+              Linked to a related report
+            </span>
+          )}
+        </div>
+        <div className="report-meta">
+          <span>≡ƒÅå {report.competitionName}</span>
+          <span>≡ƒòÆ Filed {formatDate(report.createdAt)}</span>
+          {report.incidentAt && <span>≡ƒôì Incident {formatDate(report.incidentAt)}</span>}
+          <span>Reporter history: {report.reporterPriorSubstantiated} substantiated</span>
+          <span>Accused history: {report.accusedPriorSubstantiated} substantiated</span>
+        </div>
+        <p className="report-desc">{report.description}</p>
+
+        <div className="report-actions">
+          {open && (
+            <>
+              <button className="report-btn" onClick={discuss}>Discuss</button>
+              <button className="report-btn primary" onClick={() => setModal('act')}>Act</button>
+              <button className="report-btn danger" onClick={() => setModal('discard')}>Discard</button>
+            </>
+          )}
+          <Link className="report-btn" to={`/pages/comp-info.html?id=${report.competitionId}`}>View competition</Link>
+        </div>
+      </div>
+
+      <h2 style={{ fontSize: 15, color: '#e2e8f0', margin: '22px 0 10px' }}>Evidence</h2>
+      {(!report.evidence || report.evidence.length === 0) ? (
+        <div className="report-empty">No evidence attached.</div>
+      ) : (
+        <div className="evidence-list">
+          {report.evidence.map((file) => (
+            <a className="evidence-item" key={file.id} href={file.url} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">
+              {file.filename || 'Evidence'}
+            </a>
+          ))}
+        </div>
+      )}
+
+      <h2 style={{ fontSize: 15, color: '#e2e8f0', margin: '22px 0 10px' }}>Status history</h2>
+      <ul className="timeline">
+        {(report.statusHistory || []).map((entry) => (
+          <li key={entry.id}>
+            {capitalise(entry.fromStatus || 'new')} ΓåÆ <strong>{capitalise(entry.toStatus)}</strong>
+            {entry.note ? ` ΓÇö ${entry.note}` : ''}
+            <span className="t">{formatDate(entry.createdAt)} ┬╖ @{entry.actor || 'system'}</span>
+          </li>
+        ))}
+      </ul>
+
+      {report.conclusion && (
+        <>
+          <h2 style={{ fontSize: 15, color: '#e2e8f0', margin: '22px 0 10px' }}>Recorded conclusion</h2>
+          <div className="info-card">
+            <p style={{ color: '#e2e8f0', fontSize: 13, lineHeight: 1.6, margin: 0 }}>{report.conclusion}</p>
+            <p style={{ color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: 12, marginBottom: 0 }}>
+              Finding: {capitalise(report.finding)} ┬╖ Action: {capitalise(report.action)} ┬╖ Reviewed by @{report.resolvedBy} on {formatDate(report.resolvedAt)}
+            </p>
+          </div>
+        </>
+      )}
+
+      {modal === 'discard' && (
+        <DiscardModal
+          report={report}
+          onClose={() => setModal(null)}
+          onDone={() => { setModal(null); showToast('Report discarded.', 'success'); load(); }}
+        />
+      )}
+      {modal === 'act' && (
+        <PlayerDecisionModal
+          report={report}
+          onClose={() => setModal(null)}
+          onDone={() => { setModal(null); showToast('Decision recorded.', 'success'); load(); }}
+        />
+      )}
+    </main>
+  );
