+/**
+ * NEXUS ESPORTS ΓÇö Organiser Reports Dashboard
+ *
+ * Queue of participant reports for competitions this organiser runs. A report
+ * becomes a decision only after a recorded finding + action; discarding counts
+ * towards the reporter's three baseless reports in that competition.
+ */
+import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
+import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
+import { LocalReportsAPI as ReportsAPI } from '../services/local/reporting.js';
+import useReportSync from '../hooks/useReportSync.js';
+import ReportCard from '../components/ReportCard.jsx';
+import { DiscardModal, PlayerDecisionModal } from '../components/ReportActionModals.jsx';
+import { STATUS_TABS, PRIORITY_META } from '../lib/reporting.js';
+import { showToast } from '../lib/toast.js';
+import '../styles/pages/reports.css';
+
+export default function OrganizerReports() {
+  const navigate = useNavigate();
+  const [reports, setReports] = useState([]);
+  const [tab, setTab] = useState('new');
+  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
+  const [error, setError] = useState('');
+  const [discardTarget, setDiscardTarget] = useState(null);
+  const [decisionTarget, setDecisionTarget] = useState(null);
+
+  const load = useCallback(async () => {
+    setLoading(true);
+    setError('');
+    try {
+      const res = await ReportsAPI.organiser();
+      if (!res.ok) { setError(res.error || 'Unable to load reports.'); return; }
+      setReports(Array.isArray(res.data) ? res.data : []);
+    } finally {
+      setLoading(false);
+    }
+  }, []);
+
+  useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);
+  useReportSync(load);
+
+  async function discuss(report) {
+    const res = await ReportsAPI.startDiscussion(report.id);
+    if (!res.ok) { showToast(res.error || 'Unable to open the case discussion.', 'error'); return; }
+    navigate(`/pages/case-discussion.html?discussionId=${res.data.discussion.id}`);
+  }
+
+  async function bumpPriority(report, priority) {
+    const res = await ReportsAPI.priority(report.id, priority);
+    if (!res.ok) { showToast(res.error || 'Unable to update priority.', 'error'); return; }
+    load();
+  }
+
+  const counts = reports.reduce((acc, report) => {
+    acc[report.status] = (acc[report.status] || 0) + 1;
+    return acc;
+  }, {});
+
+  const filtered = tab === 'all' ? reports : reports.filter((report) => report.status === tab);
+
+  return (
+    <main className="main-content reports-page">
+      <div className="reports-head">
+        <div>
+          <h1 className="reports-title">Reports</h1>
+          <p className="reports-subtitle">
+            Participant reports for your competitions. The Community Moderator handles any report filed against
+            an organiser ΓÇö those never appear here.
+          </p>
+        </div>
+        <Link className="report-btn" to="/pages/my-activity.html">My activity</Link>
+      </div>
+
+      <div className="reports-stats">
+        {STATUS_TABS.map((status) => (
+          <div className="report-stat" key={status.key}>
+            <div className="n">{counts[status.key] || 0}</div>
+            <div className="l">{status.label}</div>
+          </div>
+        ))}
+      </div>
+
+      {error && <div className="report-inline-error">{error}</div>}
+
+      <div className="reports-tabs">
+        <button className={`reports-tab ${tab === 'all' ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => setTab('all')}>
+          All <span className="count">{reports.length}</span>
+        </button>
+        {STATUS_TABS.map((status) => (
+          <button
+            key={status.key}
+            className={`reports-tab ${tab === status.key ? 'active' : ''}`}
+            onClick={() => setTab(status.key)}
+          >
+            {status.label} <span className="count">{counts[status.key] || 0}</span>
+          </button>
+        ))}
+      </div>
+
+      {loading ? (
+        <div className="report-empty">Loading reportsΓÇª</div>
+      ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
+        <div className="report-empty">No reports in this tab.</div>
+      ) : (
+        <div className="reports-list">
+          {filtered.map((report) => (
+            <ReportCard
+              key={report.id}
+              report={report}
+              detailHref={`/pages/organizer-report-detail.html?id=${report.id}`}
+              actions={(
+                <>
+                  {['new', 'under_review', 'awaiting_response'].includes(report.status) && (
+                    <>
+                      <button className="report-btn" onClick={() => discuss(report)}>Discuss</button>
+                      <button className="report-btn primary" onClick={() => setDecisionTarget(report)}>Act</button>
+                      <button className="report-btn danger" onClick={() => setDiscardTarget(report)}>Discard</button>
+                      <select
+                        className="report-btn"
+                        value={report.priority}
+                        onChange={(e) => bumpPriority(report, e.target.value)}
+                        title="Review priority (urgency, not proof)"
+                        style={{ background: PRIORITY_META[report.priority]?.bg }}
+                      >
+                        <option value="high">High</option>
+                        <option value="medium">Medium</option>
+                        <option value="low">Low</option>
+                      </select>
+                    </>
+                  )}
+                </>
+              )}
+            />
+          ))}
+        </div>
+      )}
+
+      {discardTarget && (
+        <DiscardModal
+          report={discardTarget}
+          onClose={() => setDiscardTarget(null)}
+          onDone={() => { setDiscardTarget(null); showToast('Report discarded.', 'success'); load(); }}
+        />
+      )}
+      {decisionTarget && (
+        <PlayerDecisionModal
+          report={decisionTarget}
+          onClose={() => setDecisionTarget(null)}
+          onDone={() => { setDecisionTarget(null); showToast('Decision recorded and the participant notified.', 'success'); load(); }}
+        />
+      )}
+    </main>
+  );
+}

+/**
+ * NEXUS ESPORTS ΓÇö Report form
+ *
+ * Context-aware: the target, competition and match come from the page the user
+ * clicked (never typed by hand). Reporter identity, team IDs, priority and
+ * routing are attached by the backend.
+ */
+import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
+import { Link, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
+import { LocalReportsAPI as ReportsAPI, prepareEvidenceFile } from '../services/local/reporting.js';
+import { reasonsFor, capitalise } from '../lib/reporting.js';
+import { showToast } from '../lib/toast.js';
+import '../styles/pages/reports.css';
+
+const MAX_FILES = 5;
+const MAX_BYTES = 5 * 1024 * 1024;
+
+export default function ReportForm() {
+  const [params] = useSearchParams();
+  const compId = params.get('compId') || '';
+  const against = params.get('against') || '';
+  const targetRole = params.get('role') || 'player';
+  const matchId = params.get('matchId') || '';
+  const source = params.get('source') || 'report-button';
+
+  const [reasonCode, setReasonCode] = useState('cheating');
+  const [description, setDescription] = useState('');
+  const [incidentAt, setIncidentAt] = useState('');
+  const [evidence, setEvidence] = useState([]);
+  const [restriction, setRestriction] = useState(null);
+  const [error, setError] = useState('');
+  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
+  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(null);
+
+  const reasons = useMemo(() => reasonsFor(targetRole === 'organiser' ? 'organiser' : 'participant'), [targetRole]);
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (!reasons.some((r) => r.code === reasonCode)) setReasonCode(reasons[0]?.code || 'other');
+  }, [reasons, reasonCode]);
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (!compId) return;
+    ReportsAPI.restrictions(compId)
+      .then((res) => { if (res.ok && res.data) setRestriction(res.data); })
+      .catch(() => undefined);
+  }, [compId]);
+
+  function addFiles(event) {
+    const files = Array.from(event.target.files || []);
+    const accepted = [];
+    for (const file of files) {
+      if (!/^image\/(png|jpeg|webp)$/.test(file.type)) {
+        showToast(`${file.name}: only PNG/JPEG/WEBP images are accepted.`, 'error');
+        continue;
+      }
+      if (file.size > MAX_BYTES) {
+        showToast(`${file.name} is larger than 5 MB.`, 'error');
+        continue;
+      }
+      accepted.push(file);
+    }
+    const next = [...evidence, ...accepted].slice(0, MAX_FILES);
+    if (evidence.length + accepted.length > MAX_FILES) showToast(`Attach at most ${MAX_FILES} files.`, 'error');
+    setEvidence(next);
+    event.target.value = '';
+  }
+
+  async function submit(event) {
+    event.preventDefault();
+    setError('');
+    if (!compId || !against) {
+      setError('This form must be opened from a Report button so the competition and the reported person are known.');
+      return;
+    }
+    if (description.trim().length < 15) {
+      setError('Describe what happened (at least 15 characters).');
+      return;
+    }
+    setBusy(true);
+    try {
+      const payload = [];
+      for (const file of evidence) {
+        payload.push(await prepareEvidenceFile(file));
+      }
+      const res = await ReportsAPI.create({
+        competitionId: compId,
+        reportedUsername: against,
+        reasonCode,
+        description: description.trim(),
+        matchId: matchId || undefined,
+        incidentAt: incidentAt ? new Date(incidentAt).toISOString() : undefined,
+        sourcePage: source,
+        evidence: payload,
+      });
+      if (!res.ok) {
+        setError(res.error || 'Unable to submit this report.');
+        return;
+      }
+      setSubmitted(res.data);
+      showToast('Report submitted. A report is an allegation ΓÇö a reviewer will look into it.', 'success');
+    } catch (err) {
+      setError(err?.message || 'Unable to submit this report.');
+    } finally {
+      setBusy(false);
+    }
+  }
+
+  if (submitted) {
+    return (
+      <main className="main-content reports-page">
+        <div className="report-inline-ok">
+          Report {submitted.code} submitted. It was routed to{' '}
+          {submitted.queue === 'moderator' ? 'the Community Moderator' : 'the competition organiser'}.
+        </div>
+        <p style={{ color: '#94a3b8', fontSize: 13 }}>
+          No punishment is applied automatically. You can follow the status and any case discussion from Reports.
+        </p>
+        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10, marginTop: 14 }}>
+          <Link className="report-btn primary" to="/pages/my-reports.html">Reports</Link>
+          <Link className="report-btn" to={`/pages/comp-info.html?id=${compId}`}>Back to competition</Link>
+        </div>
+      </main>
+    );
+  }
+
+  const locked = restriction?.restricted;
+
+  return (
+    <main className="main-content reports-page">
+      <div className="reports-head">
+        <div>
+          <h1 className="reports-title">Report a problem</h1>
+          <p className="reports-subtitle">
+            Reports are allegations, not proof. The backend decides the priority and the reviewer ΓÇö you never
+            choose who judges the case.
+          </p>
+        </div>
+        <Link className="report-btn" to="/pages/my-reports.html">Reports</Link>
+      </div>
+
+      {locked && (
+        <div className="report-inline-error">
+          You have reached the limit of 3 baseless reports in this competition, so reporting is locked here.
+          The restriction is per competition and cannot be reset by rejoining a team.
+        </div>
+      )}
+
+      <form onSubmit={submit} style={{ maxWidth: 720 }}>
+        {error && <div className="report-inline-error">{error}</div>}
+
+        <div className="report-form-grid">
+          <div className="report-field">
+            <label>Against</label>
+            <input value={against ? `@${against}` : ''} readOnly disabled />
+            <span className="hint">Filled in automatically from the page you reported from.</span>
+          </div>
+          <div className="report-field">
+            <label>Report type</label>
+            <input value={capitalise(targetRole)} readOnly disabled />
+            <span className="hint">Determined by the reported person's role in this competition.</span>
+          </div>
+        </div>
+
+        <div className="report-field">
+          <label htmlFor="reason">Reason</label>
+          <select id="reason" value={reasonCode} onChange={(e) => setReasonCode(e.target.value)}>
+            {reasons.map((reason) => (
+              <option key={reason.code} value={reason.code}>{reason.label}</option>
+            ))}
+          </select>
+        </div>
+
+        <div className="report-field">
+          <label htmlFor="description">What happened?</label>
+          <textarea
+            id="description"
+            rows={6}
+            maxLength={4000}
+            value={description}
+            onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)}
+            placeholder="Include what you saw, when it happened, and any relevant match or team context."
+          />
+          <span className="hint">{description.trim().length}/15 characters minimum</span>
+        </div>
+
+        <div className="report-form-grid">
+          <div className="report-field">
+            <label htmlFor="incident">Incident date/time <span className="hint">Optional</span></label>
+            <input id="incident" type="datetime-local" value={incidentAt} onChange={(e) => setIncidentAt(e.target.value)} />
+          </div>
+          <div className="report-field">
+            <label htmlFor="evidence">Evidence <span className="hint">Optional, up to 5 images (5 MB each)</span></label>
+            <input id="evidence" type="file" accept="image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp" multiple onChange={addFiles} />
+          </div>
+        </div>
+
+        {evidence.length > 0 && (
+          <div className="evidence-list">
+            {evidence.map((file, index) => (
+              <span className="evidence-item" key={`${file.name}-${index}`}>
+                {file.name}
+                <button
+                  type="button"
+                  className="report-btn ghost"
+                  style={{ padding: '2px 6px' }}
+                  onClick={() => setEvidence(evidence.filter((_, i) => i !== index))}
+                >
+                  Γ£ò
+                </button>
+              </span>
+            ))}
+          </div>
+        )}
+
+        <div className="report-modal-actions" style={{ justifyContent: 'flex-start', marginTop: 20 }}>
+          <button type="submit" className="report-btn primary" disabled={busy || locked}>
+            {busy ? 'SubmittingΓÇª' : 'Submit report'}
+          </button>
+          <button
+            type="button"
+            className="report-btn ghost"
+            onClick={() => { setDescription(''); setEvidence([]); setIncidentAt(''); setError(''); }}
+          >
+            Discard
+          </button>
+        </div>
+      </form>
+    </main>
+  );
+}

+/**
+ * NEXUS ESPORTS ΓÇö Community Moderator Activity
+ *
+ * Reuses the Competition Admin Activity layout: account summary card, stat
+ * counters, audit timeline cards, expandable details and the same empty state.
+ * Data comes from the local report store, filtered to resolved/discarded
+ * moderator-queue reports handled by the selected Community Moderator.
+ */
+import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
+import { Link, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
+import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext.jsx';
+import { findAccount } from '../../services/auth.js';
+import { LocalReportsAPI } from '../../services/local/reporting.js';
+import useReportSync from '../../hooks/useReportSync.js';
+import { capitalise, formatDate, reportCode } from '../../lib/reporting.js';
+import '../../styles/pages/admin/dashboard.css';
+
+const isSuperAdmin = (session) => ['super-admin', 'super_admin'].includes(String(session?.role || '').toLowerCase());
+
+const STATUS_META = {
+  resolved: { label: 'RESOLVED', icon: 'Γ£à', bg: 'rgba(34,197,94,0.15)', color: '#22c55e' },
+  discarded: { label: 'DISCARDED', icon: '≡ƒùæ∩╕Å', bg: 'rgba(148,163,184,0.15)', color: '#94a3b8' },
+};
+
+export default function ModeratorActivity() {
+  const [params] = useSearchParams();
+  const { session } = useAuth();
+  const targetUsername = (params.get('admin') || session?.username || '').trim();
+  const targetAccount = useMemo(() => findAccount(targetUsername), [targetUsername]);
+  const displayName = targetAccount?.displayName || targetUsername || 'Community Moderator';
+  const email = targetAccount?.email || (targetUsername.includes('@') ? targetUsername : `${targetUsername}@nexus.gg`);
+
+  const [rows, setRows] = useState([]);
+  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
+  const [error, setError] = useState('');
+  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState(new Set());
+
+  const load = useCallback(async () => {
+    if (!targetUsername) {
+      setError('No Community Moderator was selected.');
+      setRows([]);
+      setLoading(false);
+      return;
+    }
+    setLoading(true);
+    setError('');
+    const res = await LocalReportsAPI.moderatorActivity(targetUsername);
+    if (!res.ok) {
+      setError(res.error || 'Unable to load this moderatorΓÇÖs activity.');
+      setRows([]);
+    } else {
+      setRows(Array.isArray(res.data) ? res.data : []);
+    }
+    setLoading(false);
+  }, [targetUsername]);
+
+  useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);
+  useReportSync(load);
+
+  const toggle = (key) => setExpanded((prev) => {
+    const next = new Set(prev);
+    if (next.has(key)) next.delete(key);
+    else next.add(key);
+    return next;
+  });
+
+  const resolvedCount = rows.filter((row) => row.status === 'resolved').length;
+  const discardedCount = rows.filter((row) => row.status === 'discarded').length;
+  const superAdminView = isSuperAdmin(session);
+
+  return (
+    <main className="admin-main">
+      <header className="admin-header" style={{ marginBottom: 24 }}>
+        <div>
+          {superAdminView && (
+            <Link
+              to="/pages/super-admin/admins.html"
+              style={{ display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, color: 'var(--accent, #c6ff33)', textDecoration: 'none', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 14 }}
+            >
+              ΓåÉ Back to Administrator Directory
+            </Link>
+          )}
+          <h1 className="admin-title">Moderator <span style={{ color: 'var(--accent,#c6ff33)' }}>Activity Logs</span></h1>
+          <p className="admin-desc">Resolved and discarded organiser reports handled by this Community Moderator.</p>
+        </div>
+      </header>
+
+      <div id="admin-info-card" style={{ background: '#0a0a0a', border: '1px solid #262626', borderRadius: 16, padding: 24, marginBottom: 28, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 20 }}>
+        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 16 }}>
+          <div id="admin-avatar" style={{ width: 56, height: 56, borderRadius: '50%', background: 'rgba(244,114,182,0.15)', border: '2px solid rgba(244,114,182,0.4)', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', fontSize: 22, fontWeight: 900, color: '#f472b6' }}>
+            {String(displayName || 'M').charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
+          </div>
+          <div>
+            <h2 id="admin-username-title" style={{ margin: '0 0 4px', color: '#ffffff', fontSize: 20, fontWeight: 800 }}>{displayName}</h2>
+            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
+              <span id="admin-role-badge" style={{ padding: '4px 10px', borderRadius: 20, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 700, background: 'rgba(244,114,182,0.15)', color: '#f472b6', border: '1px solid rgba(244,114,182,0.3)' }}>Community Moderator</span>
+              <span id="admin-email-text" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)', fontSize: 13 }}>@{targetUsername || 'ΓÇö'} ┬╖ {email}</span>
+            </div>

+

+            <div style={{ fontSize: 11, color: 'var(--text-muted)', fontWeight: 700, textTransform: 'uppercase', marginBottom: 4 }}>Resolved</div>
+            <div id="stat-resolved-count" style={{ fontSize: 20, fontWeight: 900, color: '#22c55e' }}>{resolvedCount}</div>
+          </div>
+          <div style={{ background: '#141414', border: '1px solid #262626', borderRadius: 12, padding: '12px 18px', textAlign: 'center', minWidth: 110 }}>
+            <div style={{ fontSize: 11, color: 'var(--text-muted)', fontWeight: 700, textTransform: 'uppercase', marginBottom: 4 }}>Discarded</div>

+          </div>
+          <div style={{ background: '#141414', border: '1px solid #262626', borderRadius: 12, padding: '12px 18px', textAlign: 'center', minWidth: 110 }}>
+            <div style={{ fontSize: 11, color: 'var(--text-muted)', fontWeight: 700, textTransform: 'uppercase', marginBottom: 4 }}>Total Cases</div>
+            <div id="stat-total-count" style={{ fontSize: 20, fontWeight: 900, color: '#c6ff33' }}>{rows.length}</div>
+          </div>
+        </div>
+      </div>
+
+      <section style={{ background: '#0a0a0a', border: '1px solid #262626', borderRadius: 16, padding: 28 }}>
+        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 24, flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 12 }}>
+          <h3 style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 17, fontWeight: 800, color: '#ffffff', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10 }}><span>≡ƒÜ⌐ Report Activity Timeline</span></h3>
+          <div id="log-count-text" style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600 }}>
+            Showing {rows.length} activity record{rows.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} for {targetUsername || 'this moderator'}
+          </div>
+        </div>
+
+        {error && (
+          <div style={{ padding: '14px 16px', background: 'rgba(239,68,68,0.12)', border: '1px solid rgba(239,68,68,0.4)', borderRadius: 10, color: '#f87171', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600, marginBottom: 16 }}>
+            {error}
+          </div>
+        )}
+
+        {loading ? (
+          <div style={{ padding: '40px 20px', textAlign: 'center', color: 'var(--text-muted)', fontSize: 13 }}>Loading activityΓÇª</div>
+        ) : (
+          <>
+            <div id="activity-timeline-list" style={{ display: rows.length ? 'flex' : 'none', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 16 }}>
+              {rows.map((row, index) => {
+                const meta = STATUS_META[row.status] || STATUS_META.resolved;
+                const handledAt = row.status === 'discarded' ? row.discardedAt : row.resolvedAt;
+                const key = row.id || `activity-${index}`;
+                const isExpanded = expanded.has(key);
+                const evidence = Array.isArray(row.evidence) ? row.evidence : [];
+                const canOpenReport = !superAdminView;
+
+                return (
+                  <div key={key} style={{ background: '#141414', border: '1px solid #262626', borderRadius: 12, padding: 20, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 12 }}>
+                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'flex-start', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 10 }}>
+                      <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10 }}>
+                        <span style={{ fontSize: 20 }}>{meta.icon}</span>
+                        <span style={{ padding: '4px 10px', borderRadius: 20, fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 800, background: meta.bg, color: meta.color, border: `1px solid ${meta.color}33`, letterSpacing: '0.5px' }}>{meta.label}</span>
+                        <span style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)', fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 600 }}>{reportCode(row)}</span>
+                      </div>
+                      <span style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)', fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 600, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 4 }}>≡ƒòÆ {handledAt ? formatDate(handledAt) : 'Recently'}</span>
+                    </div>
+
+                    <div style={{ color: '#ffffff', fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 600, lineHeight: 1.5 }}>
+                      {capitalise(row.reasonCode)} ┬╖ @{row.reporterUsername} ΓåÆ @{row.reportedUsername}
+                    </div>
+                    <div style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)', fontSize: 12, display: 'flex', gap: 14, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
+                      {row.competitionName && <span>≡ƒÅå {row.competitionName}</span>}
+                      <span>ΓÜí {capitalise(row.priority)} priority</span>
+                      {row.status === 'resolved' && <span>Finding: {capitalise(row.finding)}</span>}

'rgba(198,255,51,0.08)' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.04)', cursor: 'pointer' }}

'Discarded on' : 'Resolved on'}</div><div style={{ color: '#ffffff', fontWeight: 600 }}>{handledAt ? formatDate(handledAt) : 'ΓÇö'}</div></div>

+                            </div>
+                          )}

+                                ))}
+                              </div>
+                            </div>
+                          )}

+                            {canOpenReport ? (
+                              <Link to={`/pages/moderator/report-detail.html?id=${encodeURIComponent(row.id)}`} className="btn-table-primary" style={{ display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6, padding: '7px 16px', fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 800, borderRadius: 6, background: '#c6ff33', color: '#000', textDecoration: 'none' }}>
+                                Open report Γåù
+                              </Link>
+                            ) : <span style={{ fontSize: 11, color: '#64748b' }}>Read-only administrator view</span>}
+                            <button type="button" className="btn-table-secondary" onClick={() => toggle(key)} style={{ padding: '6px 14px', fontSize: 12 }}>Close Γ£ò</button>
+                          </div>
+                        </div>
+                      )}
+                    </div>
+                  </div>
+                );
+              })}
+            </div>

+                <p style={{ margin: 0, color: 'var(--text-muted)', fontSize: 13 }}>This Community Moderator has no resolved or discarded reports yet.</p>
+              </div>
+            )}
+          </>
+        )}
+      </section>
+    </main>
+  );
+}

+/**
+ * NEXUS ESPORTS ΓÇö Community Moderator Profile
+ *
+ * Uses the same layout, components and account actions as the existing Admin
+ * Profile: hero/avatar, account information, security, notification
+ * preferences, recent activity and a working logout button.
+ */
+import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
+import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
+import { useAuth } from '../../context/AuthContext.jsx';
+import { findAccount, updateAccountProfile, changeAccountPassword } from '../../services/auth.js';
+import { LocalReportsAPI } from '../../services/local/reporting.js';
+import { showToast } from '../../lib/toast.js';
+import '../../styles/pages/admin/admin-profile.css';
+import '../../styles/pages/reports.css';
+
+const normalize = (value) => String(value || '').trim().toLowerCase();
+const PREF_PREFIX = 'nexus.profile.toggle.';
+const AVATAR_PREFIX = 'nexus.profile.avatar.';
+
+function readStorage(key) { try { return localStorage.getItem(key); } catch (e) { return null; } }
+function writeStorage(key, value) { try { localStorage.setItem(key, value); } catch (e) { /* ignore */ } }
+
+function initials(name) {
+  return String(name || 'M').split(' ').filter(Boolean).map((word) => word[0]).join('').toUpperCase().slice(0, 2);
+}
+
+function formatTime(value) {
+  if (!value) return '';
+  const date = new Date(value);
+  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return '';
+  return date.toLocaleString('en-IN', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', hour12: false });
+}
+
+const PREF_ITEMS = [
+  ['pref-report-new', 'New reports'],
+  ['pref-report-response', 'Awaiting responses'],
+  ['pref-report-resolved', 'Resolved cases'],
+  ['pref-email-alerts', 'Email alerts'],
+];
+
+export default function ModeratorProfile() {
+  const navigate = useNavigate();
+  const { session, logout, refreshSession } = useAuth();
+
+  const account = useMemo(() => findAccount(session?.username) || null, [session?.username]);
+  const username = account?.username || session?.username || '';
+  const avatarKey = `${AVATAR_PREFIX}${normalize(username)}`;
+
+  const [displayName, setDisplayName] = useState(account?.displayName || session?.displayName || username || 'Community Moderator');
+  const [nameField, setNameField] = useState(account?.displayName || session?.displayName || '');
+  const [email, setEmail] = useState(account?.email || session?.email || '');
+  const [usernameField] = useState(username ? `@${username}` : '');
+  const [region, setRegion] = useState('South Asia');
+  const [bio, setBio] = useState('Community Moderator responsible for reviewing reports against organisers and keeping competition cases fair and auditable.');
+  const [avatar, setAvatar] = useState(() => readStorage(avatarKey) || null);
+  const [prefs, setPrefs] = useState(() => {
+    const next = {};
+    PREF_ITEMS.forEach(([key]) => { next[key] = readStorage(PREF_PREFIX + key) !== '0'; });
+    return next;
+  });
+  const [pwdCurrent, setPwdCurrent] = useState('');
+  const [pwdNew, setPwdNew] = useState('');
+  const [pwdConfirm, setPwdConfirm] = useState('');
+  const [showPwd, setShowPwd] = useState({ current: false, new: false, confirm: false });
+  const [reports, setReports] = useState([]);
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    let alive = true;
+    LocalReportsAPI.moderation()
+      .then((res) => { if (alive && res.ok && Array.isArray(res.data)) setReports(res.data); })
+      .catch(() => undefined);
+    return () => { alive = false; };
+  }, []);
+
+  useEffect(() => { setAvatar(readStorage(avatarKey) || null); }, [avatarKey]);
+
+  const stats = useMemo(() => ({
+    assigned: reports.length,
+    active: reports.filter((row) => ['new', 'under_review', 'awaiting_response'].includes(row.status)).length,
+    resolved: reports.filter((row) => row.status === 'resolved').length,
+    discarded: reports.filter((row) => row.status === 'discarded').length,
+  }), [reports]);
+
+  const activity = useMemo(() => (
+    reports
+      .filter((row) => row.status === 'resolved')
+      .sort((a, b) => new Date(b.resolvedAt || b.updatedAt || b.createdAt) - new Date(a.resolvedAt || a.updatedAt || a.createdAt))
+      .slice(0, 6)
+      .map((row) => ({
+        id: row.id,
+        title: `${row.code || 'Report'} ┬╖ ${String(row.finding || 'resolved').replace(/_/g, ' ')} ┬╖ ${String(row.action || 'no action').replace(/_/g, ' ')}`,
+        time: row.resolvedAt || row.updatedAt || row.createdAt,
+      }))
+  ), [reports]);
+
+  const lastLogin = formatTime((session && (session.lastLoginAt || session.loggedInAt)) || Date.now());
+  const joined = account?.createdAt || session?.joinedAt
+    ? new Date(account?.createdAt || session?.joinedAt).toLocaleDateString('en-IN', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' })
+    : 'ΓÇö';
+
+  function saveAccountInfo() {
+    const name = nameField.trim();
+    if (!name) { showToast('Name cannot be empty.', 'error'); return; }
+    const result = updateAccountProfile(username, { displayName: name, email: email.trim() });
+    if (!result.ok) { showToast(result.error, 'error'); return; }
+    setDisplayName(name);
+    setEmail(result.account.email);
+    if (typeof refreshSession === 'function') refreshSession();
+    showToast('Account information saved!');
+  }
+
+  function updatePassword() {
+    if (!pwdCurrent) { showToast('Please enter your current password.', 'error'); return; }
+    if (!pwdNew || pwdNew.length < 6) { showToast('New password must be at least 6 characters.', 'error'); return; }
+    if (pwdNew !== pwdConfirm) { showToast('New passwords do not match.', 'error'); return; }
+    const result = changeAccountPassword(username, pwdCurrent, pwdNew);
+    if (!result.ok) { showToast(result.error, 'error'); return; }
+    setPwdCurrent(''); setPwdNew(''); setPwdConfirm('');
+    setShowPwd({ current: false, new: false, confirm: false });
+    showToast('Password updated successfully!');
+  }
+
+  function previewAvatar(event) {
+    const file = event.target.files[0];
+    if (!file) return;
+    const reader = new FileReader();
+    reader.onload = (ev) => {
+      const value = String(ev.target.result || '');
+      setAvatar(value);
+      writeStorage(avatarKey, value);
+    };
+    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
+  }
+
+  function togglePref(key) {
+    setPrefs((prev) => {
+      const next = { ...prev, [key]: !prev[key] };
+      writeStorage(PREF_PREFIX + key, next[key] ? '1' : '0');
+      return next;
+    });
+  }
+
+  function doLogout() {
+    logout();
+    navigate('/pages/login.html', { replace: true });
+  }
+
+  const pwdField = (id, label, value, setValue, key) => (
+    <div className="form-group">
+      <label className="form-label">{label}</label>
+      <div className="password-input-wrap">
+        <input className="form-input" type={showPwd[key] ? 'text' : 'password'} id={id} placeholder={label.toLowerCase()} value={value} onChange={(e) => setValue(e.target.value)} />
+        <button type="button" className="pwd-eye-btn" onClick={() => setShowPwd((s) => ({ ...s, [key]: !s[key] }))} aria-label="Show/hide password">{showPwd[key] ? '≡ƒÖê' : '≡ƒæü∩╕Å'}</button>
+      </div>
+    </div>
+  );
+
+  return (
+    <main className="main-content">
+      <div className="admin-header-block">
+        <h1 className="admin-title-xl">Community Moderator Profile</h1>
+        <p className="admin-subtitle-muted">Manage your account information and settings.</p>
+      </div>
+
+      <div className="profile-layout">
+        <div className="profile-sidebar-col">
+          <div className="profile-avatar-card">
+            <div className="avatar-wrap">
+              <div className="avatar-circle" id="avatar-display">{avatar ? <img src={avatar} alt="Avatar" /> : initials(displayName)}</div>
+              <button className="avatar-edit-btn" onClick={() => document.getElementById('moderator-avatar-upload')?.click()}>Γ£Å∩╕Å</button>
+              <input type="file" id="moderator-avatar-upload" accept="image/*" className="hidden-file-input" onChange={previewAvatar} />
+            </div>
+            <div className="profile-name-lg" id="display-name">{displayName}</div>
+            <div className="profile-role-badge">Community Moderator</div>
+            <div className="profile-since">Member since {joined}</div>
+            <div className="profile-status-row"><span className="status-dot-green"></span><span className="profile-status-txt">Active</span></div>
+          </div>
+
+          <div className="comp-sidebar-block">
+            <h3>Quick Stats</h3>
+            <div className="info-row"><span className="key">Assigned Reports</span><span className="val stat-val-accent">{stats.assigned}</span></div>
+            <div className="info-row"><span className="key">Active Cases</span><span className="val stat-val-accent">{stats.active}</span></div>
+            <div className="info-row"><span className="key">Resolved</span><span className="val stat-val-accent">{stats.resolved}</span></div>
+            <div className="info-row"><span className="key">Discarded</span><span className="val stat-val-accent">{stats.discarded}</span></div>
+            <div className="info-row"><span className="key">Last Login</span><span className="val">{lastLogin || 'ΓÇö'}</span></div>
+            <button type="button" className="btn-table-secondary quick-logout-btn" id="moderator-profile-logout-btn" onClick={doLogout}>Log Out</button>
+          </div>
+        </div>
+

+/**
+ * NEXUS ESPORTS ΓÇö Community Moderator report investigation
+ *
+ * Four tabs: Organiser info, Competition info, Communication & evidence, and an
+ * Activity timeline generated from real audit/status records (never fabricated).
+ */
+import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
+import { Link, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
+import { LocalReportsAPI as ReportsAPI } from '../../services/local/reporting.js';
+import useReportSync from '../../hooks/useReportSync.js';
+import ReputationBadge from '../../components/ReputationBadge.jsx';
+import { DiscardModal, ModeratorDecisionModal } from '../../components/ReportActionModals.jsx';
+import { PRIORITY_META, capitalise, formatDate, formatINR, reportCode } from '../../lib/reporting.js';
+import { showToast } from '../../lib/toast.js';
+import '../../styles/pages/admin/dashboard.css';
+import '../../styles/pages/reports.css';
+
+const TABS = [
+  { key: 'organiser', label: 'Organiser' },
+  { key: 'competition', label: 'Competition' },
+  { key: 'communication', label: 'Communication & Evidence' },
+  { key: 'timeline', label: 'Activity Timeline' },
+];
+
+export default function ModeratorReportDetail() {
+  const [params] = useSearchParams();
+  const id = params.get('id') || '';
+  const [tab, setTab] = useState('organiser');
+  const [data, setData] = useState(null);
+  const [error, setError] = useState('');
+  const [modal, setModal] = useState(null);
+
+  const load = useCallback(async () => {
+    if (!id) { setError('No report selected.'); return; }
+    const res = await ReportsAPI.investigation(id);

+  useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);

+

+/**
+ * NEXUS ESPORTS ΓÇö Community Moderator Reports
+ * Reports against organisers, with the five queue tabs and decision actions.
+ */
+import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
+import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
+import { LocalReportsAPI as ReportsAPI } from '../../services/local/reporting.js';
+import useReportSync from '../../hooks/useReportSync.js';
+import ReportCard from '../../components/ReportCard.jsx';
+import { DiscardModal, ModeratorDecisionModal } from '../../components/ReportActionModals.jsx';
+import { STATUS_TABS } from '../../lib/reporting.js';
+import { showToast } from '../../lib/toast.js';
+import '../../styles/pages/admin/dashboard.css';
+import '../../styles/pages/reports.css';
+
+// Resolved/discarded reports live in Activity, not in the active Reports queue.
+const ACTIVE_STATUS_KEYS = ['new', 'under_review', 'awaiting_response'];
+const ACTIVE_TABS = STATUS_TABS.filter((status) => ACTIVE_STATUS_KEYS.includes(status.key));
+
+export default function ModeratorReports() {
+  const navigate = useNavigate();
+  const [reports, setReports] = useState([]);
+  const [tab, setTab] = useState('new');
+  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
+  const [error, setError] = useState('');
+  const [discardTarget, setDiscardTarget] = useState(null);
+  const [decisionTarget, setDecisionTarget] = useState(null);
+
+  const load = useCallback(async () => {
+    setLoading(true);
+    setError('');
+    const res = await ReportsAPI.moderation();
+    if (!res.ok) setError(res.error || 'Unable to load organiser reports.');
+    else setReports(Array.isArray(res.data) ? res.data : []);
+    setLoading(false);
+  }, []);
+
+  useEffect(() => { load(); }, [load]);
+  useReportSync(load);
+
+  async function discuss(report) {
+    const res = await ReportsAPI.startDiscussion(report.id);
+    if (!res.ok) { showToast(res.error || 'Unable to open the case discussion.', 'error'); return; }
+    navigate(`/pages/case-discussion.html?discussionId=${res.data.discussion.id}`);
+  }
+
+  const activeReports = reports.filter((report) => ACTIVE_STATUS_KEYS.includes(report.status));
+  const counts = activeReports.reduce((acc, report) => {
+    acc[report.status] = (acc[report.status] || 0) + 1;
+    return acc;
+  }, {});
+  const filtered = tab === 'all' ? activeReports : activeReports.filter((report) => report.status === tab);
+
+  return (
+    <main className="admin-page reports-page">
+      <div className="admin-header-block">
+        <h1 className="admin-title-xl">Organiser reports</h1>
+        <p className="admin-subtitle-muted">
+          Reports about organisers. Findings and sanctions are recorded with a reason and are auditable.
+        </p>
+      </div>
+
+      {error && <div className="report-inline-error">{error}</div>}
+
+      <div className="reports-tabs">
+        <button className={`reports-tab ${tab === 'all' ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => setTab('all')}>
+          All <span className="count">{activeReports.length}</span>
+        </button>
+        {ACTIVE_TABS.map((status) => (
+          <button
+            key={status.key}
+            className={`reports-tab ${tab === status.key ? 'active' : ''}`}
+            onClick={() => setTab(status.key)}
+          >
+            {status.label} <span className="count">{counts[status.key] || 0}</span>
+          </button>
+        ))}
+      </div>
+
+      {loading ? (
+        <div className="report-empty">Loading reportsΓÇª</div>
+      ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
+        <div className="report-empty">No reports in this tab.</div>
+      ) : (
+        <div className="reports-list">
+          {filtered.map((report) => (
+            <ReportCard
+              key={report.id}
+              report={report}
+              detailHref={`/pages/moderator/report-detail.html?id=${report.id}`}
+              actions={['new', 'under_review', 'awaiting_response'].includes(report.status) ? (
+                <>
+                  <button className="report-btn" onClick={() => discuss(report)}>Discuss</button>
+                  <button className="report-btn primary" onClick={() => setDecisionTarget(report)}>Act</button>
+                  <button className="report-btn danger" onClick={() => setDiscardTarget(report)}>Discard</button>
+                </>
+              ) : null}
+            />
+          ))}
+        </div>
+      )}
+
+      {discardTarget && (
+        <DiscardModal
+          report={discardTarget}
+          onClose={() => setDiscardTarget(null)}
+          onDone={() => { setDiscardTarget(null); showToast('Report discarded.', 'success'); load(); }}
+        />
+      )}
+      {decisionTarget && (
+        <ModeratorDecisionModal
+          report={decisionTarget}
+          onClose={() => setDecisionTarget(null)}
+          onDone={() => { setDecisionTarget(null); showToast('Decision recorded.', 'success'); load(); }}
+        />
+      )}
+    </main>
+  );
+}

+// ΓöÇΓöÇ Local administrative account management ΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇΓöÇ
+// All administrator and Community Moderator accounts use the same
+// `nexus.auth.accounts` store as every other local account, so login, logout,
+// role normalisation and the existing auth context keep working unchanged.
+
+function upsertStoredAccount(account) {
+  const stored = readAccountsFromStorage();
+  const key = normalize(account.username);
+  const index = stored.findIndex((entry) => normalize(entry.username) === key);
+  if (index >= 0) stored[index] = { ...stored[index], ...account };
+  else stored.push(account);
+  saveAccountsToStorage(stored);
+  return account;
+}
+
+function sameIdentity(a, b) {
+  return normalize(a) === normalize(b) && normalize(a) !== '';
+}
+
+export function findAccount(identity) {
+  const key = normalize(identity);
+  if (!key) return null;
+  return getAccounts().find((entry) => normalize(entry.username) === key || normalize(entry.email) === key) || null;
+}
+
+/** Every administrator account, including seed accounts and Community Moderators. */
+export function listAdministrativeAccounts() {
+  return getAccounts().filter(isAdministrativeAccount);
+}
+
+export function createAdministrator(options = {}) {
+  const fullName = String(options.fullName || options.displayName || '').trim();
+  const username = String(options.username || '').trim().replace(/^@/, '');
+  const email = String(options.email || '').trim();
+  const password = String(options.password || '');
+  const confirm = options.confirmPassword === undefined ? password : String(options.confirmPassword || '');
+  const meta = resolveAdminRole(options.role);
+
+  if (!fullName || !username || !email || !password) {
+    return { ok: false, error: 'Full name, username, email and password are required.' };
+  }
+  if (fullName.length < 2) return { ok: false, error: 'Enter the administratorΓÇÖs full name.' };
+  if (username.length < 3) return { ok: false, error: 'Username must be at least 3 characters.' };
+  if (!EMAIL_RE.test(email)) return { ok: false, error: 'Enter a valid email address.' };
+  if (password.length < 6) return { ok: false, error: 'Password must be at least 6 characters.' };
+  if (confirm !== password) return { ok: false, error: 'Password and confirmation do not match.' };
+  if (!meta) return { ok: false, error: 'Select a valid administrative role.' };
+  if (meta.role === 'participant') return { ok: false, error: 'This form only creates administrative accounts.' };
+
+  const accounts = getAccounts();
+  const usernameKey = normalize(username);
+  const emailKey = normalize(email);
+  if (accounts.some((entry) => normalize(entry.username) === usernameKey)) {
+    return { ok: false, error: 'That username is already in use.' };
+  }
+  if (accounts.some((entry) => normalize(entry.email) === emailKey)) {
+    return { ok: false, error: 'That email address is already in use.' };
+  }
+
+  const account = {
+    username,
+    email,
+    password,
+    role: meta.role,
+    adminType: meta.adminType,
+    displayName: fullName,
+    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
+    status: 'active',
+    banned: false,
+    createdBy: String(options.createdBy || '').trim() || null,
+  };
+  upsertStoredAccount(account);
+  return { ok: true, account };
+}
+
+/**
+ * Mirrors the original Add Admin workflow: create a new account or elevate an
+ * existing participant. New accounts use the same default password as the
+ * platform seed accounts (`nexus`) and can sign in immediately.
+ */
+export function addOrElevateAdministrator(options = {}) {
+  const uname = String(options.username || '').trim().replace(/^@/, '');
+  if (!uname) return { ok: false, error: 'Please enter a username or email' };
+
+  const email = String(options.email || (uname.includes('@') ? uname : `${uname}@nexus.gg`)).trim();
+  const meta = resolveAdminRole(options.adminType);
+  if (!meta) return { ok: false, error: 'Select a valid administrative role.' };
+
+  const existing = findAccount(uname) || findAccount(email);
+  if (existing) {
+    if (isAdministrativeAccount(existing)) {
+      return { ok: false, error: 'User is already an admin' };
+    }
+    const next = {
+      ...existing,
+      role: meta.role,
+      adminType: meta.adminType,
+      email: existing.email || email,
+      revokedReason: null,
+      updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
+    };
+    upsertStoredAccount(next);
+    return { ok: true, account: next, elevated: true };
+  }
+
+  const account = {
+    username: uname,
+    email,
+    password: 'nexus',
+    role: meta.role,
+    adminType: meta.adminType,
+    displayName: uname,
+    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
+    status: 'active',
+    banned: false,
+    createdBy: String(options.actor || '').trim() || null,
+  };
+  upsertStoredAccount(account);
+  return { ok: true, account, created: true };
+}
+
+export function updateAdministrator(identity, updates = {}) {
+  const existing = findAccount(identity);
+  if (!existing) return { ok: false, error: 'Administrator not found.' };
+
+  const next = { ...existing };
+
+  if (updates.displayName !== undefined) {
+    const name = String(updates.displayName || '').trim();
+    if (!name) return { ok: false, error: 'Display name cannot be empty.' };
+    next.displayName = name;
+  }
+
+  if (updates.email !== undefined) {
+    const email = String(updates.email || '').trim();
+    if (!EMAIL_RE.test(email)) return { ok: false, error: 'Enter a valid email address.' };
+    const clash = getAccounts().some((entry) => (
+      !sameIdentity(entry.username, existing.username) && normalize(entry.email) === normalize(email)
+    ));
+    if (clash) return { ok: false, error: 'That email address is already in use.' };
+    next.email = email;
+  }
+
+  if (updates.role !== undefined) {
+    const meta = resolveAdminRole(updates.role);
+    if (!meta) return { ok: false, error: 'Select a valid administrative role.' };
+    next.role = meta.role;
+    next.adminType = meta.adminType;
+  }
+
+  if (updates.status !== undefined) {
+    const status = String(updates.status || '').toLowerCase() === 'inactive' ? 'inactive' : 'active';
+    next.status = status;
+    next.banned = status === 'inactive';
+  }
+
+  upsertStoredAccount(next);
+
+  if (sameIdentity(getSession()?.username, existing.username)) {
+    const current = getSession();
+    if (current) {
+      setSession({
+        ...current,
+        displayName: next.displayName || current.displayName,
+        email: next.email || current.email,
+        role: next.role || current.role,
+        adminType: next.adminType || current.adminType,
+      });
+    }
+  }
+  return { ok: true, account: next };
+}
+
+export function setAdministratorActive(identity, active, actor) {
+  if (!active && sameIdentity(identity, actor)) {
+    return { ok: false, error: 'You cannot deactivate your own account.' };
+  }
+  return updateAdministrator(identity, { status: active ? 'active' : 'inactive' });
+}
+
+export function revokeAdministrator(identity, options = {}) {
+  const existing = findAccount(identity);
+  if (!existing) return { ok: false, error: 'Administrator not found.' };
+  if (sameIdentity(existing.username, options.actor)) {
+    return { ok: false, error: 'You cannot revoke your own administrator access.' };
+  }
+  const next = {
+    ...existing,
+    role: 'participant',
+    adminType: null,
+    status: 'active',
+    banned: false,
+    revokedReason: String(options.reason || '').trim(),
+    revokedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
+    revokedBy: String(options.actor || '').trim() || null,
+  };
+  upsertStoredAccount(next);
+  return { ok: true, account: next };
+}
+
+export function updateAccountProfile(identity, updates = {}) {
+  const existing = findAccount(identity);
+  if (!existing) return { ok: false, error: 'Account not found.' };
+  const next = { ...existing };
+  if (updates.displayName !== undefined) {
+    const name = String(updates.displayName || '').trim();
+    if (!name) return { ok: false, error: 'Name cannot be empty.' };
+    next.displayName = name;
+  }
+  if (updates.email !== undefined) {
+    const email = String(updates.email || '').trim();
+    if (!EMAIL_RE.test(email)) return { ok: false, error: 'Enter a valid email address.' };
+    const clash = getAccounts().some((entry) => (
+      !sameIdentity(entry.username, existing.username) && normalize(entry.email) === normalize(email)
+    ));
+    if (clash) return { ok: false, error: 'That email address is already in use.' };
+    next.email = email;
+  }
+  upsertStoredAccount(next);
+  if (sameIdentity(getSession()?.username, existing.username)) {
+    const current = getSession();
+    if (current) setSession({ ...current, displayName: next.displayName, email: next.email });
+  }
+  return { ok: true, account: next };
+}
+
+export function changeAccountPassword(identity, currentPassword, nextPassword) {
+  const existing = findAccount(identity);
+  if (!existing) return { ok: false, error: 'Account not found.' };
+  if (String(existing.password || '') !== String(currentPassword || '')) {
+    return { ok: false, error: 'Current password is incorrect.' };
+  }
+  const next = String(nextPassword || '');
+  if (next.length < 6) return { ok: false, error: 'New password must be at least 6 characters.' };
+  upsertStoredAccount({ ...existing, password: next });
+  return { ok: true };
+}
